57	Run by the reviewer agent over what it is given (base, package, tasks, paths): the interface review after Phase 0, a phase review per package at each checkpoint, and the milestone exit. `dt land` already checked each landing's `Writes:`, protected paths and gates; review judges what tools can't.
58	
59	**Interface** (after Phase 0): the code is the contract, no more and no less.
60	1. Types match `contract.md`: fields, types, invariants. Nothing a step's In → Out needs is missing; no helper is frozen.
61	2. Signatures match the step tables, in their owner modules. Each stub names the open task that owns its step; no logic beyond types and validation.
62	3. One sample per case the contract names, valid and realistically shaped, never carrying a TC's expected value. Replay doubles replay an interaction the Protocol allows; fakes serve their fixtures.
63	4. `__init__.py` exports exactly the contract; each `Mitigated by` in the plan's dependency graph exists; each split task's tester can write its tests from the frozen interface and its `Read:`.
64	
65	**Each phase**, per package, tests before code:
66	1. **Interfaces followed** (CR-09): no frozen type, signature or contract folder changed outside a Revise. Consumers use the declared types: no dict for a dataclass, no reaching into a provider's internals, no local redefinition. A stub is replaced only by the task it names; no cross-package import outside the contract; spine tests run on samples and doubles, not another step's code.
67	2. **Tests prove something:** each intent test asserts its TC case or acceptance bullet, loading expected values and tolerances from fixtures; none is assertion-free or weakened; Hypothesis runs the `ci` profile. Unit tests are specific, never computing the expected value with the code under test or testing only a mock. **Mutation check** (`.claude/agents/reviewer.md`): per acceptance bullet, the smallest change that should break it; a mutation no test catches is a finding.
68	3. **Correct to the spec:** each acceptance bullet truly met, not special-cased to the sample; the error paths the requirement names; seeding and determinism; numerical edge cases.
69	4. **Simple** (CR-11, CR-12, CR-42): no abstraction with one implementation outside the contract or an AD; no forwarding wrapper, one-method class, or class where a function does; no parameter, flag or config key every caller sets the same; no `try/except` that only re-raises or swallows, no checks the types already guarantee; no loop the library already provides. No near-copies within or across the phase's tasks: one concept is one function in its owner module (tests may repeat for readability). Each finding names the simpler form, with the same tests passing.
70	5. **Pipelines** (CR-10): entry functions read as their step tables (`dt layout`); step owners respected; spine baselines still selectable.
71	6. **The bar** (CR-07): diff this file, `pyproject.toml`, `scripts/` and `.claude/agents/` against the milestone's Base. Any loosening is Critical; new exceptions need an owner and expiry.
72	7. **Hidden and parallel risks:** where a real provider could act differently from its double (flagged for the system phase); fixed output paths, global state, test-order dependence.
73	8. **The rest:** readability, security, performance where a QR sets a number; reused reference or probe code has its Caveats handled and fits the contract; `dt lint` clean, pre-build sections replaced by `dt layout` pointers.
74	
75	**Milestone exit**, over the whole diff, adds: the Scope's Exit holds; every Covers ID has a test; `check.sh full --exit` finds no stub; system tests use real providers; the same sample through a real provider and its double agrees; deferred requirements untouched; pip-audit ran.
76	
77	**Severity:** Critical: a frozen interface changed, a weakened test, an intent test that misses a mutation changing its TC's expected output, a wrong answer, a loosened bar, a security hole, lost data; it stops the build. Important: a consumer bypassing a type, any other surviving mutation, an unneeded abstraction, a duplicate across tasks. Suggestion and Nit: local. Findings go under the checkpoint block in `tasks/plan.md` (`dt finding`), each with a fix task or a reason.

57	## PL-03: Training batch
58	**Entry:** `src/rllab/train/pipelines/pl03_batch.py::run_batch` · **Trigger:** `rllab batch <config.toml> --seeds 1-5` (and `rllab train`, one seed)
59	**Covers:** TRAIN-05, TRAIN-06, QR-02
60	
61	| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
62	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
63	| 1 | `load_run_configs` | train.config | config path, seeds → list[RunConfig] | PL-01 as built | TRAIN-01 |
64	| 2 | `check_plan` | train.check | list[RunConfig] → MethodConfig per method | PL-01 as built | TRAIN-06 |
65	| 3 | `train_checks` | train.check | MethodConfig per method, seed → Learner per method | PL-01 as built | TRAIN-06 |
66	| 4 | `score_checks` | train.check | Learner per method, MethodConfig per method → list[CheckResult] | PL-01 as built | TRAIN-06 |
67	| 5 | `require_pass` | train.check | list[CheckResult] → list[CheckResult] | raise `CheckFailedError` on the first failure | TRAIN-06 |
68	| 6 | `run_parallel` | train.batch | list[RunConfig], root, workers → list[RunRecord] | `spawn` process pool, one `run_train` (PL-02 as built) per config, one torch thread each (AD-05) | TRAIN-05, QR-02 |
69	
70	### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
71	```python
72	def run_batch(config_path: Path, seeds: Sequence[int], root: Path, *, workers: int | None = None, check_seed: int = 0) -> list[RunRecord]:
73	    configs = load_run_configs(config_path, seeds)
74	    plan = check_plan(configs)
75	    learners = train_checks(plan, check_seed)
76	    results = score_checks(learners, plan)
77	    require_pass(results)
78	    return run_parallel(configs, root, workers)
79	```

104	| 2 | `baseline_means` | baselines.means | world, variant → BaselineMeans | random and rule per team on the 20 `EVAL_SEEDS`, rule-based opponents | EVAL-01, EVAL-02 |

106	| 4 | `SnakeRule` | baselines.snake | none → RulePolicy | toward food on the shorter axis unless fatal next step | SNAKE-07 |
107	| 5 | `FishRule` | baselines.fish | none → RulePolicy | full push away from the seen ball, else random | FISH-09 |
108	| 6 | `PreyRule` | baselines.prey | none → RulePolicy | flee nearest seen predator, else toward nearest seen pellet | PREY-07 |
109	| 7 | `PredatorRule` | baselines.prey | none → RulePolicy | chase nearest seen prey, else random wander | PREY-07 |
110	| 8 | `learning_curve` | charts.curve | list[Run], BaselineMeans, out → Output | matplotlib mean line, min–max band, two baseline lines, per team; `dataviz` checks | LOG-03–LOG-05, PREY-10 |
111	
112	### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
113	```python
114	def run_chart(root: Path, world: str, variant: str, out: Path, *, include: Sequence[str] = (), measure: str | None = None) -> Output:
115	    runs = find_runs(root, world, variant, include=include)
116	    lines = baseline_means(world, variant)
117	    return learning_curve(runs, lines, out, measure=measure)
118	```

122	**Entry:** `src/rllab/cli/pipelines/pl06_evaluate.py::run_evaluate` · **Trigger:** `rllab evaluate <world> <variant> [--include NAME]`
123	**Covers:** EVAL-03–EVAL-06, SNAKE-08, FISH-10, PREY-08, QR-05
124	
125	| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
126	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
127	| 1 | `find_runs` | runlog.read | root, world, variant, include → list[Run] | PL-05 as built | EVAL-03 |
128	| 2 | `final_evaluation` | evaluate.final | list[Run], out → list[Summary] | serial play of final checkpoints and both baselines on `FINAL_SEEDS`; `compare` per baseline with the `null-hypothesis-testing` skill, Holm, EVAL-05 verdict, `summary.json` | EVAL-03–EVAL-06, PREY-08, QR-05 |
129

131	```python
132	def run_evaluate(root: Path, world: str, variant: str, out: Path, *, include: Sequence[str] = (), workers: int | None = None) -> list[Summary]:
133	    runs = find_runs(root, world, variant, include=include)
134	    return final_evaluation(runs, out, workers=workers)

138	## PL-08: Replay video
139	**Entry:** `src/rllab/cli/pipelines/pl08_video.py::run_video` · **Trigger:** `rllab video <world> <variant> <side> --seed N --out FILE [--sight]`
140	**Covers:** REPLAY-07, REPLAY-02, QR-06
141	
142	| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
143	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
144	| 1 | `open_sources` | replay.sources | world, variant, side spec → Checkpoint or baseline per team | as `open_sides`, one side | REPLAY-07 |
145	| 2 | `make` | worlds.make | world, variant → World | PL-02 as built | WORLD-06 |
146	| 3 | `policies_for` | replay.sources | Checkpoint or baseline per team, WorldSpec → Policy per team | `methods.load_policy` or `baselines` per team; a team left out takes the first checkpoint's | REPLAY-01, REPLAY-06 |
147	| 4 | `play_episode` | core.episode | World, Policy per team, seed → Episode | reset, act per team, step until done or `max_steps`, frames kept | REPLAY-02, WORLD-02 |
148	| 5 | `to_trace` | replay.tracing | Episode, WorldSpec, label → Trace | copy frames, set label and step-limit flag | REPLAY-02 |
149	| 6 | `save_video` | replay.video | Trace, out → Output | draw each frame off-screen, imageio-ffmpeg H.264 at 30 fps | REPLAY-07, QR-06 |
150	
151	### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
152	```python
153	def run_video(world: str, variant: str, side: str, seed: int, out: Path, *, overlay: bool = False) -> Output:
154	    sources = open_sources(world, variant, side)
155	    w = make(world, variant)
156	    policies = policies_for(sources, w.spec)
157	    episode = play_episode(w, policies, seed, trace=True)
158	    trace = to_trace(episode, w.spec, side)
159	    return save_video(trace, out, overlay_sight=overlay)
160	```

163	### core.ActionSpace: type
164	`ActionSpace = Discrete(n: int) | Box(dims: int, low: float, high: float)`, both frozen dataclasses
165	- **Invariants:** `Discrete` actions are ints in `[0, n)`; `Box` actions are `dims` floats in `[low, high]`
166	- **Boundary:** worlds, baselines, methods, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.ActionSpace/ · **Cases:** snake `Discrete(3)`; fish `Box(2, -1.0, 1.0)`
167	- **Used by:** worlds, baselines, methods, refcheck

172	- **Invariants:** `agents` are indices into the world's agent axis; teams of one world are disjoint and cover every agent
173	- **Boundary:** worlds, train, evaluate, charts · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.TeamSpec/ · **Cases:** snake's one team; predator-prey's prey (6) and predator (2) teams
174	- **Used by:** every component reading scores

177	### core.WorldSpec: type
178	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class WorldSpec: world: str; variant: str; agents: tuple[str, ...]; teams: tuple[TeamSpec, ...]; obs_len: int; action: ActionSpace; max_steps: int; measures: tuple[MeasureSpec, ...]; sight: float | None`
179	`MeasureSpec(name: str, team: str, direction: Direction)`
180	- **Invariants:** available without running an episode (WORLD-04); `sight` is the radius in world units for local-sight variants, else `None`; equality of two specs is field by field
181	- **Boundary:** worlds, methods, runlog, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.WorldSpec/ · **Cases:** snake default; fish local-sight (8 agents, sight set); predator-prey

185	### core.Step: type
186	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Step: obs: Array; rewards: Array; active: Array; done: bool; truncated: bool`
187	- **Invariants:** `obs` float32 `n_agents × obs_len`; `rewards` float32 `n_agents`; `active` bool `n_agents`, and an inactive agent's row is zeros with reward 0 (WORLD edge case); `truncated` is true only when `max_steps` ended the episode
188	- **Boundary:** worlds, methods, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Step/ · **Cases:** a snake mid-episode step; a predator-prey step with one prey caught

194	- **Invariants:** `scores` and `team_rewards` keyed by team name (WORLD-05, LOG-02); `measures` keyed by every `MeasureSpec.name`
195	- **Boundary:** train, runlog, evaluate, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.EpisodeResult/ · **Cases:** a snake episode with 12 food; a predator-prey episode with both team scores

220	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:59 -->
221	### core.World: type
222	```python
223	class World(Protocol):
224	    spec: WorldSpec
225	    def reset(self, seed: int) -> Step: ...
226	    def step(self, actions: Array) -> Step: ...
227	    def result(self) -> EpisodeResult: ...
228	    def draw(self) -> DrawState: ...
229	```
230	- **Invariants:** same seed → same start (WORLD-01); same seed and actions → same steps (WORLD-02); `actions` has one row per agent, rows of inactive agents ignored
231	- **Errors:** `step` raises `ActionError(agent: str, value: object)`, a `ValueError`, for an action outside `spec.action`, never clipping it; `step` after `done` raises `RuntimeError`
232	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.World/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded 5-step snake episode from seed 42
233	- **Used by:** every component that plays episodes; PL-02, PL-08
234	
235	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:73 -->
236	### core.Policy: type
237	`class Policy(Protocol): def act(self, obs: Array, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Array`
238	- **Invariants:** `obs` is `k × obs_len` for the `k` agents of one team; returns `k` actions inside the world's `ActionSpace`; deterministic for a given `obs` and `rng` state
239	- **Boundary:** baselines, methods, evaluate, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Policy/ · **Cases:** a replay double of the snake rule policy over 5 observations
240	- **Used by:** core.play_episode, train, evaluate, replay, refcheck; PL-08
241

243	### core.play_episode: The one episode loop
244	`def play_episode(world: World, policies: Mapping[str, Policy], seed: int, *, trace: bool = False) -> Episode`
245	- **Invariants:** `policies` keyed by team; the seed spawns the world's and each policy's generator (AD-05), so the same inputs give the same `Episode`; stops at `done` or `spec.max_steps`
246	- **Errors:** `KeyError` naming a team with no policy; `ActionError` propagates from `World.step`
247	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.play_episode/ · **Cases:** snake rule policy, seed 7, traced
248	- **Used by:** train, evaluate, replay, refcheck; PL-02, PL-08

334	### methods.Learner: type
335	```python
336	class Learner(Protocol):
337	    def act(self, obs: Array, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Array: ...   # exploring
338	    def observe(self, t: Transition) -> None: ...                       # updates on its own schedule
339	    def policy(self) -> Policy: ...                                      # frozen, deterministic copy
340	    def state_dict(self) -> dict[str, object]: ...
341	```
342	- **Invariants:** hand-written learning code only (CHECK-01); `policy()` is greedy (DQN) or the mean action (PPO) and does not change as the learner trains
343	- **Errors:** `observe` raises `NonFiniteError(value: float)` when a loss or parameter is NaN or infinite, before changing any parameter
344	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/methods.Learner/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a DQN learner over 10 snake transitions
345	- **Used by:** train; PL-01, PL-02, PL-03
346

348	### methods.make_learner: Learner factory
349	`def make_learner(config: MethodConfig, spec: WorldSpec, team: str, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Learner`
350	- **Invariants:** `dqn` needs a `Discrete` action space and `ppo` a `Box` one
351	- **Errors:** `ValueError` naming the method and the action format on a mismatch
352	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/methods.make_learner/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the recorded DQN learner double
353	- **Used by:** train; PL-02

358	- **Invariants:** the same checkpoint and team give a policy that acts identically every time
359	- **Errors:** `SpecMismatchError` naming each differing field (observation length, action format) when `ckpt.spec` doesn't match `spec` (REPLAY edge case)
360	- **Boundary:** evaluate, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/methods.load_policy/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the snake rule policy for any snake checkpoint
361	- **Used by:** evaluate, replay; PL-08

367	### train.load_run_configs: Config loader
368	`def load_run_configs(path: Path, seeds: Sequence[int]) -> list[RunConfig]`
369	- **Invariants:** one config per seed, named `<file stem>-s<seed>`; method settings missing from the TOML take the method's defaults
370	- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` for a missing file; `ValueError` naming an unknown key, world, variant or method
371	- **Boundary:** refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.load_run_configs/ · **Cases:** a fake returning snake configs for seeds 1 and 2

375	### train.run_check: Method check
376	`def run_check(config_path: Path, seed: int = 0) -> list[CheckResult]`
377	- **Invariants:** one result per distinct team method in the config, trained on its check world (`check-discrete` for `dqn`, `check-continuous` for `ppo`) with `check_settings`, for the fixed check budget; under a minute on the laptop (TRAIN-06, AD-09)
378	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.run_check/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the passing DQN check

381	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:215 -->
382	### train.run_train: One training run
383	`def run_train(config: RunConfig, root: Path) -> RunRecord`
384	- **Invariants:** exactly `budget` world steps (TRAIN-01); checkpoints at 0%, 10%, … 100% (TRAIN-03); evaluation of every team on `EVAL_SEEDS` at every 5% (TRAIN-04); same config on the same machine → identical logs and checkpoints (TRAIN-02)
385	- **Errors:** `RunExistsError` before any work if the run folder exists; `NonFiniteError` ends the run as `failed` with step and value, keeping the last good checkpoint; interruption marks it `incomplete`
386	- **Boundary:** PL-03 · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.run_train/ · **Cases:** a fake writing the finished 3-checkpoint snake run folder
387	- **Used by:** train.run_batch; PL-02, PL-03

389	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:222 -->
390	### train.run_batch: Training batch
391	`def run_batch(config_path: Path, seeds: Sequence[int], root: Path, *, workers: int | None = None, check_seed: int = 0) -> list[RunRecord]`
392	- **Invariants:** loads one config per seed, runs the method check with `check_seed` before any run, then one process per config up to `workers` (default: the core count), each with one torch thread (TRAIN-05, TRAIN-06, AD-05)
393	- **Errors:** `CheckFailedError(result: CheckResult)` before any run starts; a failing run doesn't stop the others
394	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.run_batch/ · **Cases:** a fake returning 2 finished snake records
395	- **Used by:** cli; PL-03

400	- **Invariants:** `agreement` is the share of check-world states where the trained policy picks the best action; `passed` is `agreement >= 0.95` (TRAIN-06)
401	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.CheckResult/ · **Cases:** a passing DQN check; a failing PPO check at 0.80

405	### runlog.RunRecord: type
406	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class RunRecord: config: RunConfig; spec: WorldSpec; started: datetime; ended: datetime | None; status: Literal["running", "finished", "incomplete", "failed"]; failure: Failure | None; path: Path`
407	`Failure(step: int, value: float, checkpoint: Path)`
408	- **Invariants:** every LOG-01 field present once the run ends; `config` is a `core.RunConfig` and `config.methods` holds full settings, defaults included; every field type comes from `core`, so `runlog` imports `core` only (AD-12)
409	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, charts, refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runlog.RunRecord/ · **Cases:** a finished snake run; a failed run with a NaN at step 41,200; an incomplete run

412	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:244 -->
413	### runlog.EvalPoint: type
414	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class EvalPoint: step: int; scores: Mapping[str, float]; measures: Mapping[str, float]`
415	- **Invariants:** means over the 20 `EVAL_SEEDS` episodes, per team and per measure; a finished run has 21, at 0%, 5%, … 100% (TRAIN-04)
416	- **Boundary:** charts, evaluate, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runlog.EvalPoint/ · **Cases:** snake point at 50%; predator-prey point with both teams
417	- **Used by:** charts, evaluate, cli
418	
419	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:250 -->
420	### runlog.Checkpoint: type
421	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Checkpoint: run: str; pct: int; step: int; spec: WorldSpec; methods: Mapping[str, MethodConfig]; state: Mapping[str, dict[str, object]]; path: Path`
422	- **Invariants:** `pct` in {0, 10, …, 100}; `methods` holds `core.MethodConfig`s (AD-12); `state` keyed by team; saved and loaded through `torch.save`/`torch.load(weights_only=True)`
423	- **Boundary:** methods, evaluate, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runlog.Checkpoint/ · **Cases:** the 0% and 100% checkpoints of a short snake run
424	- **Used by:** methods, evaluate, replay; PL-07, PL-08
425	
426	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:256 -->
427	### runlog.Run: type
428	```python
429	class Run(Protocol):
430	    record: RunRecord
431	    def episodes(self) -> list[EpisodeResult]: ...
432	    def evals(self) -> list[EvalPoint]: ...
433	    def checkpoints(self) -> list[Checkpoint]: ...
434	```
435	- **Invariants:** read-only view of one run folder; readable while the run writes (LOG-06)
436	- **Boundary:** charts, evaluate, replay, refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runlog.Run/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a finished 3-checkpoint snake run folder
437	- **Used by:** charts, evaluate, replay, refcheck, cli; PL-05, PL-06, PL-12
438	
439	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:268 -->
440	### runlog.RunWriter: Run folder writer
441	```python
442	class RunWriter:
443	    def __init__(self, root: Path, config: RunConfig, spec: WorldSpec) -> None: ...
444	    def episode(self, result: EpisodeResult) -> None: ...
445	    def eval(self, point: EvalPoint) -> None: ...      # flushes every log
446	    def checkpoint(self, pct: int, step: int, state: Mapping[str, dict[str, object]]) -> Checkpoint: ...
447	    def close(self, status: str, failure: Failure | None = None) -> RunRecord: ...
448	```
449	- **Invariants:** `config` is a `core.RunConfig` (AD-12)
450	- **Errors:** `RunExistsError` naming the path if `runs/<world>/<variant>/<name>` exists (TRAIN edge case)
451	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runlog.RunWriter/ · **Cases:** a replay double of the writes of a 3-checkpoint snake run
452	- **Used by:** train; PL-02
453	
454	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:282 -->
455	### runlog.find_runs: Run lookup
456	`def find_runs(root: Path, world: str, variant: str, *, include: Sequence[str] = ()) -> list[Run]`
457	- **Invariants:** finished runs only, plus incomplete or failed runs named in `include` (TRAIN edge case); a `running` run with no live process is reported as incomplete
458	- **Boundary:** charts, evaluate, refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runlog.find_runs/ · **Cases:** a fake returning 5 finished snake runs plus one named incomplete run
459	- **Used by:** charts, evaluate, refcheck, cli; PL-05, PL-06
460	
461	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:288 -->
462	### runlog.open_run: Open one run
463	`def open_run(path: Path) -> Run`
464	- **Invariants:** any status, including `running`, `incomplete` and `failed`
465	- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` if no `run.json` is at `path`
466	- **Boundary:** replay, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runlog.open_run/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the finished 3-checkpoint snake run double
467	- **Used by:** replay, cli; PL-12
468	
469	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:295 -->
470	### runlog.Progress: type
471	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Progress: step: int; budget: int; latest: EvalPoint | None; elapsed: timedelta; status: str`
472	- **Invariants:** at most one evaluation point behind the run (LOG-06)
473	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runlog.Progress/ · **Cases:** a run at 35% with the 35% point written
474	- **Used by:** cli; PL-04

476	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:301 -->
477	### runlog.progress: Live run view
478	`def progress(path: Path) -> Progress`
479	- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` if no run is at `path`
480	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runlog.progress/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the 35% progress
481	- **Used by:** cli; PL-04
482	
483	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:309 -->
484	### charts.learning_curve: Learning-curve chart
485	`def learning_curve(runs: Sequence[Run], baselines: BaselineMeans, out: Path, *, measure: str | None = None) -> Output`
486	- **Invariants:** draws per team a mean line, a min–max band and both baseline lines against "environment steps" (LOG-03, PREY-10), or a declared measure the same way (LOG-05); follows the `dataviz` skill (LOG-04); under 3 runs, titled with the count and a band note
487	- **Errors:** `MixedRunsError` naming the differing settings when runs differ by more than seed
488	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/charts.learning_curve/ · **Cases:** a fake writing a placeholder PNG for 5 snake runs
489	- **Used by:** cli; PL-05
490	
491	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:324 -->
492	### evaluate.Comparison: type
493	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Comparison: baseline: Literal["random", "rule"]; team: str; learned_mean: float; baseline_mean: float; difference: float; p_raw: float; p_holm: float; rejected: bool`
494	- **Invariants:** `difference` is signed so positive means better in the team's direction
495	- **Boundary:** refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.Comparison/ · **Cases:** a rejected snake-vs-random; a kept snake-vs-rule
496	- **Used by:** evaluate.Summary
497	
498	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:330 -->
499	### evaluate.compare: Paired permutation test
500	`def compare(learned: Array, baseline: Array, direction: Direction, *, permutations: int = 10_000, rng: np.random.Generator) -> tuple[float, float]`
501	- **Invariants:** `learned` is runs × seeds, averaged over runs per seed; `baseline` has one score per seed, on the same seeds; swaps labels within each seed; returns (difference, one-sided p) as EVAL-04 states; built and checked with the `null-hypothesis-testing` skill
502	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if seed counts differ
503	- **Used by:** evaluate.final_evaluation
504	
505	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:336 -->
506	### evaluate.Summary: type
507	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Summary: world: str; variant: str; team: str; runs: tuple[str, ...]; learned_mean: float; learned_range: tuple[float, float]; run_means: Mapping[str, float]; random_mean: float; rule_mean: float; comparisons: tuple[Comparison, ...]; verdict: Verdict; collapse: int | None`
508	`Verdict(beats_baselines: bool, failing_runs: tuple[str, ...], runs_needed: int)`
509	- **Invariants:** one per world, variant and team (EVAL-06, PREY-08); `comparisons` empty and `runs_needed > 0` with fewer than 5 runs; `collapse` is set when the team's in-training eval score stays at or below its random-baseline mean for the last 4+ consecutive eval points, and is the step of the first of them
510	- **Boundary:** charts, refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.Summary/ · **Cases:** a passing snake summary; one with a failing run; one with 3 runs
511	- **Used by:** refcheck, cli; PL-06
512	
513	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:343 -->
514	### evaluate.final_evaluation: Final evaluation
515	`def final_evaluation(runs: Sequence[Run], out: Path, *, workers: int | None = None) -> list[Summary]`
516	- **Invariants:** plays every final checkpoint and both baselines on `FINAL_SEEDS` (EVAL-03); Holm across the 2 comparisons per team; a pass needs both nulls rejected (EVAL-04) and every run's mean beating the rule mean (EVAL-05); in predator-prey each team plays against the rule-based opponent (PREY-08); within 10 minutes (QR-05)
517	- **Boundary:** refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.final_evaluation/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the passing snake summary
518	- **Used by:** refcheck, cli; PL-06
519	
520	<!-- docs/packages/rllab/contract.md:466 -->
521	### cli.main: The rllab command
522	`def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int`
523	- **Invariants:** subcommands `check`, `train`, `batch`, `watch`, `chart`, `evaluate`, `grid`, `refcheck`, `replay`, `video`, `notes new`, `notes check`, each dispatched to its pipeline entry (`train` is a one-seed `batch`); prints the entry's returned contract type; owns no pipeline step (AD-10); every error is printed as one line naming its cause, exit code 1
524	- **Used by:** the learner

# Single package. build-setup writes [build-system], [dependency-groups] and every [tool.*]
# section; [project] dependencies come from the plan (Extend adds each milestone's).
# CONSTRAINTS.md cites these keys: change a number in both, through build-setup.
# references/ (reference files from docs/references.md) and probes/ (probe scripts from
# docs/probes.md) are not project code: every tool below excludes them, they are protected, and
# nothing imports from them (CR-08).
[project]
name = "rllab"
version = "0.1.0"
requires-python = ">=3.12"
dependencies = [
    "numpy>=2.1",
    "torch>=2.5",
    "matplotlib>=3.9",
]

[build-system]
requires = ["uv_build>=0.11,<0.12"]
build-backend = "uv_build"

[dependency-groups]
dev = [
    "ruff",
    "mypy",
    "pyright",
    "pytest",
    "pytest-cov",
    "hypothesis",
    "deptry",
    "vulture",
    "pylint",
    "typos",
    "import-linter",
    "pip-audit",

]

# ================================================================ Ruff (CR-20, CR-21)
[tool.ruff]
line-length = 100
target-version = "py312"
src = ["src", "tests"]
output-prefer-rule-codes = true # preview mode prints rule names; noqa and CONSTRAINTS.md use codes
extend-exclude = ["scripts/vulture_whitelist.py", "references", "probes", ".claude", "docs"]  # vendored skill scripts; Python blocks in the docs
force-exclude = true            # excluded even when a path is passed explicitly (check.sh fast)

[tool.ruff.lint]
preview = true                  # needed for PLR0914 / PLR1702 / PLR0904 below
explicit-preview-rules = true   # ...but only turn on preview rules named here

select = [
    # Core bug-finders and modernisers
    "E", "F", "I", "B", "UP", "SIM", "C4", "RUF",
    # Quality
    "A", "RET", "PTH", "PERF", "PGH", "G", "TRY",
    "PLE", "PLW",               # only the Pylint error/warning groups
    # Security and tests
    "S", "PT",
    # Domain (from the tech stack ADs)
    "DTZ", "NPY",
    # Complexity and size
    "C901",                     # cyclomatic complexity
    "PLR0911",                  # too many returns
    "PLR0912",                  # too many branches
    "PLR0913",                  # too many arguments
    "PLR0915",                  # too many statements
    "PLR0917",                  # too many positional arguments
    "PLR0914",                  # too many locals          (preview)
    "PLR1702",                  # too many nested blocks   (preview)
    "PLR0904",                  # too many public methods  (preview)
]

    "E501",     # line length is handled by the formatter
    "TRY003",   # long messages in exception constructors: too noisy
]

# Standing entries (CR-20). Any other entry is an exception: one row in CONSTRAINTS.md's table.

"tests/**"    = ["S101", "S105", "S106",                      # asserts and fake secrets are fine
                 "C901", "PLR0912", "PLR0913", "PLR0915", "PLR0917"]  # fixtures and parametrize are long

[tool.ruff.lint.isort]

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# One entry point for every quality check. Hooks call it; so do CI and people.
# Written by dev-team2 build-setup. Change it through build-setup (Revise), not by hand:
# CONSTRAINTS.md names the rule each step enforces.
#
#   scripts/check.sh fast FILE...        per edit, instant               (PostToolUse: tester, implementer)
#   scripts/check.sh collect             tester's finish gate            (Stop: tester)
#   scripts/check.sh gate [--pkg ID]     implementer's finish gate       (Stop: implementer and solo; dt land)
#   scripts/check.sh full [--pkg ID]     checkpoints, CI                 (reviewer)
#   scripts/check.sh full --exit         the milestone exit: also no interface stub left
#   scripts/check.sh protected           print the protected-path globs  (path_guard.sh deny --protected)
#
# The base is refs/worktree/checks-base, one per worktree (dt claim sets it in a builder's worktree,
# dt land in the main checkout), else HEAD.
#
# tests/contracts/** in the protected list means the boundary contracts frozen at the base (CR-09);
# a contract folder new since the base belongs to the interface task freezing it, which may write it,
# and so does a frozen folder the task's .claude/.task_writes names (a re-freeze interface task).
#
# collect runs the intent tests changed since the base and fails unless they are red for the right
# reason (scripts/red_check.py): AssertionError or an interface stub's NotImplementedError, never
# an ImportError or AttributeError.
#
# --pkg ID runs the tests of package ID and of every package that depends on it (multi-package:
# deptry too); gate and full also run tests/contracts, whole, when it exists. Lint, types, size
# and boundaries always cover the whole repo; they take seconds.
# FILE paths are absolute or relative to the project root.
# Prints every failure (it does not stop at the first) and exits 1 if any step failed.
set -u
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." || exit 1   # hooks may run from another directory
export NO_COLOR=1                   # plain output for the agent reading it

# ---- Generated by build-setup. Do not edit by hand. ----------------------------------------
# The protected list (CONSTRAINTS.md, "Roles and protected paths"). One list serves as git
# pathspecs here and as path_guard.sh globs; in both, * also matches /.
PROTECTED=('tests/intent/**' 'tests/fixtures/**' 'tests/conftest.py' 'pyproject.toml' 'uv.lock' 'CONSTRAINTS.md' 'scripts/**' '.claude/agents/**' '.claude/settings.json' 'references/**' 'probes/**' 'tests/contracts/**')
SRC_DIRS=(src)            # single package: src · multi-package: packages/<id>/src ...
PACKAGES_DIR=""    # empty for a single package; multi-package: packages
TYPE_GATE="mypy"          # mypy | pyright: the type checker that gates (CR-23); full runs both

# A package (or, in a single package, a component) and every package that depends on it,
# from the architecture's dependency rules.
pkg_scope() {
  case "$1" in
    core) echo "core worlds baselines methods train runlog charts evaluate replay refcheck cli" ;;
    worlds) echo "worlds baselines train charts evaluate replay refcheck cli" ;;
    baselines) echo "baselines train charts evaluate replay refcheck cli" ;;
    methods) echo "methods train charts evaluate replay refcheck cli" ;;
    train) echo "train refcheck cli" ;;
    runlog) echo "runlog methods train charts evaluate replay refcheck cli" ;;
    charts) echo "charts cli" ;;
    evaluate) echo "evaluate charts refcheck cli" ;;
    replay) echo "replay cli" ;;
    refcheck) echo "refcheck cli" ;;
    notes) echo "notes cli" ;;
    cli) echo "cli" ;;
    *) return 1 ;;
  esac
}

# Non-Python parts (CONSTRAINTS.md names them): their own check commands, run in gate and full.
other_parts() {
  : # e.g. step npm --prefix web run check
}
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

mode="${1:-gate}"
shift || true
pkg=""
exit_check=()
args=()
while [ $# -gt 0 ]; do
  case "$1" in
    --pkg) pkg="${2:-}"; shift 2 || shift ;;
    --exit) exit_check=(--exit); shift ;;
    *) args+=("$1"); shift ;;
  esac
done

if [ -n "$pkg" ] && ! pkg_scope "$pkg" >/dev/null; then
  echo "unknown package '$pkg' (see pkg_scope in scripts/check.sh)" >&2
  exit 64
fi

rc=0
failed=()

# A step that is not a Python tool (a script, git, another toolchain).
step() {
  echo "-> $*" >&2
  "$@" || { rc=1; failed+=("$*"); }
}

# A tool from the project's locked environment, never whatever is first on PATH.
run() {
  echo "-> $*" >&2
  uv run --frozen --quiet "$@" || { rc=1; failed+=("$*"); }
}

# A measured check that fails only at the milestone exit: it prints its findings, and the reviewer
# records them, but a mid-milestone run passes.
report() {
  echo "-> $* (reported; fails only with --exit)" >&2
  uv run --frozen --quiet "$@" || echo "(reported, not failing: $*)" >&2
}

# pytest exits 5 when it collects nothing. That is fine for gate and full on a project with no
# tests yet; collect calls pytest through run() so an empty tests/intent fails.
run_tests() {
  echo "-> pytest $*" >&2
  uv run --frozen --quiet pytest "$@"
  local status=$?
  [ "$status" -eq 5 ] && { echo "(no tests collected)" >&2; return; }
  [ "$status" -eq 0 ] || { rc=1; failed+=("pytest $*"); }
}

# The commit this task started from: refs/worktree/checks-base, set per worktree by `dt claim` (and
# by `dt land` in the main checkout). Without it, HEAD, which misses changes already committed.
BASE_REF="refs/worktree/checks-base"
base_commit() {
  git rev-parse -q --verify "$BASE_REF" 2>/dev/null || git rev-parse -q --verify HEAD 2>/dev/null
}

# A boundary contract is frozen once its folder exists at the base (CR-09): each folder under
# tests/contracts/ at the base, and any file directly in it. A folder new since the base is the
# interface task freezing it, so that task may write it; the next task's base freezes it. A frozen
# folder the task's .claude/.task_writes names (from its Writes:, by dt claim) is a re-freeze
# interface task's, so it is open to that task; dt land checks the Writes: against the plan.
CONTRACTS_GLOB="tests/contracts/**"
claimed_contract() {
  local line
  [ -s .claude/.task_writes ] || return 1
  while IFS= read -r line; do
    line="${line%%[[:space:]]}"
    line="${line%/}"; line="${line%/\*\*}"
    [ "$line" = "$1" ] && return 0
  done <.claude/.task_writes
  return 1
}
frozen_contracts() {
  local base meta path
  base=$(base_commit) || return 0
  git ls-tree "$base" -- tests/contracts/ 2>/dev/null | while IFS=$'\t' read -r meta path; do
    case "$meta" in
      *" tree "*) claimed_contract "$path" || echo "$path/**" ;;
      *) echo "$path" ;;
    esac
  done
}

# The protected list as enforced: PROTECTED, with tests/contracts/** narrowed to the frozen contracts.
protected_globs() {
  local g
  for g in "${PROTECTED[@]}"; do
    if [ "$g" = "$CONTRACTS_GLOB" ]; then frozen_contracts; else echo "$g"; fi
  done
}

# Fail if anything matching the given pathspecs changed since the base, untracked files included.
unchanged_since_base() {
  local label="$1" base new
  shift
  echo "-> $label unchanged since $(git rev-parse -q --verify "$BASE_REF" >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo "$BASE_REF" || echo HEAD)" >&2
  if ! base=$(base_commit); then
    echo "no commit to compare against: commit the tester's work first" >&2
    rc=1; failed+=("$label: no commit"); return
  fi
  git --no-pager diff --exit-code --stat "$base" -- "$@" >&2 || { rc=1; failed+=("$label changed"); }
  new=$(git ls-files --others --exclude-standard -- "$@")
  if [ -n "$new" ]; then
    printf 'new untracked files in %s:\n%s\n' "$label" "$new" >&2
    rc=1; failed+=("$label changed")
  fi
}

# The tester may change tests/intent only. Docs and plans are the plugin's business, not this check's.
only_intent_changed() {
  local specs=(tests "${SRC_DIRS[@]}" "${PROTECTED[@]}")
  [ -n "$PACKAGES_DIR" ] && specs+=("$PACKAGES_DIR")
  unchanged_since_base "files outside tests/intent" "${specs[@]}" ':(exclude)tests/intent'
}

# The intent test files the tester changed since the base, committed or not.
changed_intent_tests() {
  local base
  base=$(base_commit) || return 0
  { git diff --relative --name-only --diff-filter=d "$base" -- 'tests/intent/*.py'
    git ls-files --others --exclude-standard -- 'tests/intent/*.py'; } | sort -u |
    while IFS= read -r f; do case "${f##*/}" in test_*|*_test.py) echo "$f" ;; esac; done
}

# Test directories for this run: all of them, or the --pkg scope; tests/contracts always whole.
test_dirs() {
  local scope="" d p
  [ -n "$pkg" ] && scope=$(pkg_scope "$pkg")
  for d in tests/intent tests/unit; do
    if [ -z "$scope" ]; then
      [ -d "$d" ] && echo "$d"
    else
      for p in $scope; do [ -d "$d/$p" ] && echo "$d/$p"; done
    fi
  done
  [ -d tests/contracts ] && echo tests/contracts
}

# deptry checks what each package imports against what it declares.
dependencies() {
  local p scope
  if [ -z "$PACKAGES_DIR" ]; then
    run deptry . --no-ansi
    return
  fi
  if [ -n "$pkg" ]; then
    scope=$(pkg_scope "$pkg")
  else
    scope=$(cd "$PACKAGES_DIR" && for d in */pyproject.toml; do [ -f "$d" ] && dirname "$d"; done)
  fi
  for p in $scope; do
    echo "-> deptry $PACKAGES_DIR/$p" >&2
    (cd "$PACKAGES_DIR/$p" && uv run --frozen --quiet deptry . --no-ansi) || { rc=1; failed+=("deptry $PACKAGES_DIR/$p"); }
  done
}

static_checks() {
  run ruff check --ignore-noqa .   # exceptions go in per-file-ignores, which is protected (CR-01)
  run ruff format --check .
  run "$TYPE_GATE"
  run python scripts/check_size.py "${SRC_DIRS[@]}"
  dependencies
  run lint-imports --no-logo
}

case "$mode" in
  fast)
    # Only Python files, only the files just edited. Fixes what it can in place.
    files=()
    for f in ${args[@]+"${args[@]}"}; do [[ $f == *.py ]] && files+=("$f"); done
    [ ${#files[@]} -eq 0 ] && exit 0
    run ruff check --fix --quiet "${files[@]}"
    run ruff format --quiet "${files[@]}"
    run typos "${files[@]}"
    run python scripts/check_size.py "${files[@]}"
    ;;
  collect)
    if [ ! -d tests/intent ]; then
      echo "tests/intent does not exist: write the intent tests there" >&2
      exit 1
    fi
    run ruff check --ignore-noqa tests/intent
    run ruff format --check tests/intent
    run typos tests/intent
    run pytest tests/intent --collect-only -q --no-cov
    changed=()
    while IFS= read -r f; do changed+=("$f"); done < <(changed_intent_tests)
    run python scripts/red_check.py ${changed[@]+"${changed[@]}"}
    step bash scripts/floor_guard.sh
    only_intent_changed
    ;;
  gate)
    static_checks
    other_parts
    dirs=$(test_dirs)
    # shellcheck disable=SC2086  # one directory per word, by construction
    [ -n "$dirs" ] && run_tests $dirs --no-cov -q -x
    step bash scripts/floor_guard.sh
    protected=()
    while IFS= read -r g; do protected+=("$g"); done < <(protected_globs)
    unchanged_since_base "protected files" "${protected[@]}"
    ;;
  full)
    static_checks
    other_parts
    if [ "$TYPE_GATE" = mypy ]; then run pyright; else run mypy; fi
    # CR-41: the interface task's stubs and fields stay unused until later tasks use them.
    if [ ${#exit_check[@]} -gt 0 ]; then run vulture; else report vulture; fi
    run pylint --disable=all --enable=duplicate-code --recursive=y --score=n --fail-under=10 "${SRC_DIRS[@]}"   # CR-42
    run typos
    run pip-audit --skip-editable   # the external opinion: a vulnerability database (CR-26)
    step bash scripts/floor_guard.sh ${exit_check[@]+"${exit_check[@]}"}
    dirs=$(test_dirs)
    # Coverage threshold comes from [tool.coverage.report] fail_under (CR-40).
    # shellcheck disable=SC2086
    [ -n "$dirs" ] && HYPOTHESIS_PROFILE=ci run_tests $dirs --cov -q
    ;;
  protected)
    protected_globs
    ;;
  *)
    echo "unknown mode: $mode (fast | collect | gate | full | protected)" >&2
    exit 64
    ;;
esac
[ $rc -ne 0 ] && printf 'FAILED: %s\n' "${failed[@]}" >&2
exit $rc

  "file_path": "/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-a2cf415a90b125018/scripts/hooks/stop_gate.sh"

output
1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
2	# Stop hook (becomes SubagentStop inside a subagent): block finishing until the gate passes.
3	#   stop_gate.sh collect|gate|full [--warn]
4	# Blocks by printing {"decision": "block", "reason": ...} and exiting 0, not by exit 2:
5	# Claude Code treats an exit-2 Stop hook whose stderr mentions a missing file
6	# ("No such file or directory") as non-blocking, and test output often does.
7	# Loop guard: after MAX_TRIES failed attempts the agent may stop. Outside a dt build the last
8	# block tells it to start its report with "GATE FAILED", because that report is all the parent
9	# sees: a SubagentStop systemMessage doesn't reach the main view (checked live, Claude Code 2.1.292).
10	# --warn never blocks: a failing gate only leaves a systemMessage (CONSTRAINTS.md says when).
11	# For gate and full, .claude/.check_pkg (written by dt claim, optional) holds the task's package
12	# and becomes --pkg.
13	# In a dt build, .claude/.dt (written by dt claim) holds the path to dt.py: an agent whose claim
14	# has an escalation record (dt escalate) may stop without the gate, and every gate result is
15	# recorded with `dt build verdict`, which dt land reuses when the tree hasn't changed since.
16	mode=gate
17	warn=0
18	for a in "$@"; do
19	  case "$a" in
20	    --warn) warn=1 ;;
21	    *) mode="$a" ;;
22	  esac
23	done
24	MAX_TRIES=3
25	source "$(dirname "$0")/project_root.sh"
26	
27	# Without jq there is no safe way to write the JSON answer: block the plain way (exit 2,
28	# fixed message) rather than let the agent stop on a gate nobody ran.
29	say_block() {
30	  if command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
31	    jq -n --arg reason "$1" '{decision: "block", reason: $reason}'
32	    exit 0
33	  fi
34	  echo "stop_gate: the gate failed and jq is not installed to report it; install jq, then run scripts/check.sh $mode" >&2
35	  exit 2
36	}
37	say_user() {
38	  command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1 && jq -n --arg msg "$1" '{systemMessage: $msg}'
39	  exit 0
40	}
41	
42	cd "$root" || say_block "stop_gate: cannot enter the project root $root"
43	id=$(jq -r '.agent_id // .session_id // "main"' <<<"$input" 2>/dev/null | tr -cd 'A-Za-z0-9_-')
44	counter=".claude/.stop_attempts_${id:-main}"
45	mkdir -p .claude
46	
47	dt=""
48	[ -s .claude/.dt ] && dt=$(head -n 1 .claude/.dt)
49	if [ -n "$dt" ] && python3 "$dt" build escalated >/dev/null 2>&1; then
50	  rm -f "$counter"
51	  exit 0
52	fi
53	
54	pkg_args=()
55	if [ "$mode" != collect ] && [ -s .claude/.check_pkg ]; then
56	  pkg_args=(--pkg "$(tr -cd 'A-Za-z0-9_.-' <.claude/.check_pkg)")
57	fi
58	
59	out=$(bash scripts/check.sh "$mode" ${pkg_args[@]+"${pkg_args[@]}"} 2>&1)
60	rc=$?
61	[ -n "$dt" ] && python3 "$dt" build verdict --mode "$mode" --rc "$rc" >/dev/null 2>&1
62	if [ "$rc" -eq 0 ]; then
63	  rm -f "$counter"
64	  exit 0
65	fi
66	
67	if [ "$warn" = 1 ]; then
68	  say_user "Gate '$mode' is failing (warn only, not blocking). Run scripts/check.sh $mode to see why."
69	fi
70	
71	tries=$(cat "$counter" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)
72	[[ $tries =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || tries=0
73	tries=$((tries + 1))
74	echo "$tries" >"$counter"
75	if [ "$tries" -gt "$MAX_TRIES" ]; then
76	  rm -f "$counter"
77	  say_user "Gate '$mode' still failing after $MAX_TRIES attempts; the agent stopped so a human can look. Run scripts/check.sh $mode."
78	fi
79	
80	last=""
81	if [ "$tries" -eq "$MAX_TRIES" ] && [ -n "$dt" ]; then
82	  last="
83	This was the last attempt. If you can't fix it now, finish anyway with your status line:
84	the failing verdict is recorded, and dt land rejects the task with it."
85	elif [ "$tries" -eq "$MAX_TRIES" ]; then
86	  last="
87	This was the last attempt. If you can't fix it now, finish anyway and start your report with
88	\"GATE FAILED: scripts/check.sh $mode\", then list what is still failing."
89	fi
90	say_block "Gate '$mode' failed (attempt $tries/$MAX_TRIES). Fix these before finishing:
91	$(echo "$out" | tail -60)$last"

1	# rllab — Contract
2	
3	**Package:** rllab · **Status:** Approved · **Last updated:** 2026-10-18 · **Architecture:** [../../architecture.md](../../architecture.md)
4	
5	`Direction = Literal["higher", "lower"]`, `Settings = Mapping[str, int | float | str | bool]` and `Array = np.ndarray` are used throughout; `core` defines all three.
6	
7	## core
8	
9	### core.ActionSpace: type
10	`ActionSpace = Discrete(n: int) | Box(dims: int, low: float, high: float)`, both frozen dataclasses
11	- **Invariants:** `Discrete` actions are ints in `[0, n)`; `Box` actions are `dims` floats in `[low, high]`
12	- **Boundary:** worlds, baselines, methods, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.ActionSpace/ · **Cases:** snake `Discrete(3)`; fish `Box(2, -1.0, 1.0)`
13	- **Used by:** worlds, baselines, methods, refcheck
14	
15	### core.TeamSpec: type
16	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class TeamSpec: name: str; agents: tuple[int, ...]; score_name: str; direction: Direction`
17	- **Invariants:** `agents` are indices into the world's agent axis; teams of one world are disjoint and cover every agent
18	- **Boundary:** worlds, train, evaluate, charts · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.TeamSpec/ · **Cases:** snake's one team; predator-prey's prey (6) and predator (2) teams
19	- **Used by:** every component reading scores
20	
21	### core.WorldSpec: type
22	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class WorldSpec: world: str; variant: str; agents: tuple[str, ...]; teams: tuple[TeamSpec, ...]; obs_len: int; action: ActionSpace; max_steps: int; measures: tuple[MeasureSpec, ...]; sight: float | None`
23	`MeasureSpec(name: str, team: str, direction: Direction)`
24	- **Invariants:** available without running an episode (WORLD-04); `sight` is the radius in world units for local-sight variants, else `None`; equality of two specs is field by field
25	- **Boundary:** worlds, methods, runlog, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.WorldSpec/ · **Cases:** snake default; fish local-sight (8 agents, sight set); predator-prey
26	- **Used by:** all components; PL-02, PL-08
27	
28	### core.Step: type
29	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Step: obs: Array; rewards: Array; active: Array; done: bool; truncated: bool`
30	- **Invariants:** `obs` float32 `n_agents × obs_len`; `rewards` float32 `n_agents`; `active` bool `n_agents`, and an inactive agent's row is zeros with reward 0 (WORLD edge case); `truncated` is true only when `max_steps` ended the episode
31	- **Boundary:** worlds, methods, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Step/ · **Cases:** a snake mid-episode step; a predator-prey step with one prey caught
32	- **Used by:** core.play_episode, train, refcheck
33	
34	### core.EpisodeResult: type
35	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class EpisodeResult: seed: int; length: int; scores: Mapping[str, float]; team_rewards: Mapping[str, float]; measures: Mapping[str, float]`
36	- **Invariants:** `scores` and `team_rewards` keyed by team name (WORLD-05, LOG-02); `measures` keyed by every `MeasureSpec.name`
37	- **Boundary:** train, runlog, evaluate, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.EpisodeResult/ · **Cases:** a snake episode with 12 food; a predator-prey episode with both team scores
38	- **Used by:** train, runlog, evaluate, refcheck
39	
40	### core.DrawState: type
41	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class DrawState: width: float; height: float; objects: tuple[Drawable, ...]`
42	`Drawable(kind: str, x: float, y: float, size: float, team: str | None, agent: int | None)`
43	- **Invariants:** lists every object on screen at that step (WORLD-06); snake cells use grid coordinates with `size` 1; `agent` set for agents, so the viewer can draw `WorldSpec.sight` around them
44	- **Boundary:** worlds, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.DrawState/ · **Cases:** a snake board; an 8-fish tank with the ball
45	- **Used by:** replay
46	
47	### core.Frame: type
48	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Frame: step: int; draw: DrawState; scores: Mapping[str, float]; rewards: Array`
49	- **Invariants:** `scores` is each team's running score after this step; `rewards` the latest per agent (REPLAY-05); frame 0 is the reset state with zero rewards
50	- **Boundary:** replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Frame/ · **Cases:** frame 0 and frame 1 of seed 7 snake
51	- **Used by:** replay
52	
53	### core.Episode: type
54	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Episode: result: EpisodeResult; frames: tuple[Frame, ...] | None; hit_step_limit: bool`
55	- **Invariants:** `frames` is set only when traced, with `result.length + 1` entries
56	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Episode/ · **Cases:** an untraced snake episode; a traced 3-step check-world episode
57	- **Used by:** train, evaluate, replay, refcheck; PL-08
58	
59	### core.World: type
60	```python
61	class World(Protocol):
62	    spec: WorldSpec
63	    def reset(self, seed: int) -> Step: ...
64	    def step(self, actions: Array) -> Step: ...
65	    def result(self) -> EpisodeResult: ...
66	    def draw(self) -> DrawState: ...
67	```
68	- **Invariants:** same seed → same start (WORLD-01); same seed and actions → same steps (WORLD-02); `actions` has one row per agent, rows of inactive agents ignored
69	- **Errors:** `step` raises `ActionError(agent: str, value: object)`, a `ValueError`, for an action outside `spec.action`, never clipping it; `step` after `done` raises `RuntimeError`
70	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.World/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded 5-step snake episode from seed 42
71	- **Used by:** every component that plays episodes; PL-02, PL-08
72	
73	### core.Policy: type
74	`class Policy(Protocol): def act(self, obs: Array, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Array`
75	- **Invariants:** `obs` is `k × obs_len` for the `k` agents of one team; returns `k` actions inside the world's `ActionSpace`; deterministic for a given `obs` and `rng` state
76	- **Boundary:** baselines, methods, evaluate, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Policy/ · **Cases:** a replay double of the snake rule policy over 5 observations
77	- **Used by:** core.play_episode, train, evaluate, replay, refcheck; PL-08
78	
79	### core.play_episode: The one episode loop
80	`def play_episode(world: World, policies: Mapping[str, Policy], seed: int, *, trace: bool = False) -> Episode`
81	- **Invariants:** `policies` keyed by team; the seed spawns the world's and each policy's generator (AD-05), so the same inputs give the same `Episode`; stops at `done` or `spec.max_steps`
82	- **Errors:** `KeyError` naming a team with no policy; `ActionError` propagates from `World.step`
83	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.play_episode/ · **Cases:** snake rule policy, seed 7, traced
84	- **Used by:** train, evaluate, replay, refcheck; PL-02, PL-08
85	
86	### core.EVAL_SEEDS: In-training evaluation seeds
87	`EVAL_SEEDS: tuple[int, ...] = tuple(range(1_000_000, 1_000_020))`
88	- **Invariants:** 20 seeds, disjoint from training seeds `[0, 10⁶)` and from `FINAL_SEEDS` (TRAIN-04)
89	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.EVAL_SEEDS/ · **Cases:** the 20 seeds as listed
90	- **Used by:** train, evaluate; PL-02, PL-05, PL-12
91	
92	### core.FINAL_SEEDS: Final evaluation seeds
93	`FINAL_SEEDS: tuple[int, ...] = tuple(range(2_000_000, 2_000_100))`
94	- **Invariants:** 100 seeds, shared by every run, baseline and reference (EVAL-03, CHECK-02)
95	- **Boundary:** evaluate, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.FINAL_SEEDS/ · **Cases:** the 100 seeds as listed
96	- **Used by:** evaluate, refcheck; PL-06, PL-09
97	
98	### core.Output: type
99	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Output: path: Path; kind: Literal["chart", "video", "writeup"]; made_from: tuple[str, ...]`
100	- **Invariants:** `path` exists when returned; `made_from` names the runs, checkpoints and seeds behind the file, so a write-up can cite it (NOTES-02)
101	- **Boundary:** charts, replay, notes, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Output/ · **Cases:** a 5-run snake learning-curve PNG; a seed-7 snake video
102	- **Used by:** charts, replay, notes, cli; PL-05, PL-08, PL-10, PL-12
103	
104	### core.MethodConfig: type
105	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class MethodConfig: method: Literal["dqn", "ppo"]; settings: Settings`
106	- **Invariants:** `settings` holds only keys the method declares; missing keys take the method's defaults, filled by `train.load_run_configs` and written back in full to the run record; lives in `core` so `runlog` can hold it (AD-12)
107	- **Boundary:** methods, train, runlog, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.MethodConfig/ · **Cases:** snake DQN defaults; PPO with one overridden learning rate
108	- **Used by:** methods, train, runlog, refcheck; PL-01, PL-03
109	
110	### core.RunConfig: type
111	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class RunConfig: name: str; world: str; variant: str; methods: Mapping[str, MethodConfig]; seed: int; budget: int; check_settings: Mapping[str, Settings]; device: str = "cpu"`
112	- **Invariants:** `methods` has one entry per team (TRAIN-07); `budget` counts world steps, not agent steps; `check_settings` overrides settings for the check world only (AD-09); lives in `core` so `runlog` can hold it (AD-12)
113	- **Boundary:** train, runlog, refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.RunConfig/ · **Cases:** a snake config from `configs/snake-default.toml`; a predator-prey config with two methods
114	- **Used by:** train, runlog, refcheck, cli; PL-01, PL-02, PL-03, PL-09
115	
116	## worlds
117	
118	### worlds.make: World factory
119	`def make(world: str, variant: str = "default", **overrides: int | float) -> World`
120	- **Invariants:** worlds `snake` (`default`), `fish` (`one`, `many`, `local`), `prey` (`default`), `check-discrete`, `check-continuous`; `overrides` change only the spec's starting values (grid size, counts, step limits; spec Assumptions)

1	# RL Lab — Architecture
2	
3	**Status:** Approved · **Shape:** Single package · **Last updated:** 2026-10-18 · **Spec:** [spec.md](spec.md)
4	
5	Single package (`rllab`): one learner, one laptop, one install, nothing deployed. The one seam that could become its own package is `refcheck`, the only component that carries Stable-Baselines3 and Gymnasium. It sits behind an optional `check` extra, so a split would move it out as it is.
6	
7	## Overview
8	Every world implements one multi-agent `World` protocol (AD-04): it resets from a seed, steps on one action array for all agents, and reports per-team scores and a drawable state. `train` drives a hand-written learner per team (AD-03) over a world for a fixed step budget. It plays held-out evaluation episodes through `core.play_episode` and writes everything to a run folder through `runlog` (AD-06). `evaluate` replays final checkpoints and both baselines on the final seeds and runs the paired permutation test. `charts` draws learning curves from run folders. `replay` re-simulates any checkpoint or baseline from a seed into a `Trace`, then shows it in a pygame window or writes it to MP4 (AD-07). `refcheck` trains Stable-Baselines3 on the same world through an adapter, kept away from every training run (AD-08).
9	
10	```mermaid
11	flowchart LR
12	  cli --> train & evaluate & charts & replay & refcheck & notes
13	  train --> methods & runlog & worlds & baselines
14	  evaluate --> methods & runlog & worlds & baselines
15	  replay --> methods & runlog & worlds & baselines
16	  refcheck --> evaluate & train & worlds
17	  charts --> runlog & evaluate & baselines
18	  methods --> runlog
19	  worlds & baselines & methods & runlog --> core
20	```
21	
22	## Tech stack
23	| Area | Choice | Decision |
24	|---|---|---|
25	| Language, env | Python 3.12, uv, one `pyproject.toml` | AD-01 |
26	| Networks, autograd | PyTorch ≥ 2.5, CPU wheels; `nn` layers and `optim` only | AD-02 |
27	| Numerics, world physics | NumPy ≥ 2.1, vectorised over agents | AD-04 |
28	| Learning methods | Hand-written DQN (discrete) and PPO (continuous), one learner per team | AD-03 |
29	| Seeds, parallel runs | `np.random.SeedSequence`, one spawned process per run | AD-05 |
30	| Run storage | JSON record + JSON Lines logs + `torch.save` checkpoints per run folder | AD-06 |
31	| Replay window, video | pygame-ce ≥ 2.5; imageio ≥ 2.36 with imageio-ffmpeg ≥ 0.5 (bundled ffmpeg) | AD-07 |
32	| Charts | matplotlib ≥ 3.9, PNG + SVG | — |
33	| Reference check | stable-baselines3 ≥ 2.4 + gymnasium ≥ 1.0, optional extra `check` | AD-08 |
34	| Settings | TOML files in `configs/`, read with `tomllib` into frozen dataclasses owned by `core` | AD-12 |
35	| CLI | `argparse`, one `rllab` console script | — |
36	| Tests, import rules | pytest; import-linter | — |

38	## Components
39	| Component | Responsibility | Covers | Depends on |
40	|---|---|---|---|
41	| `core` | World and policy protocols, shared types (run and method settings included, AD-12), held-out seed sets, and the one episode loop every other component plays through | WORLD-01–WORLD-07 (interface), WORLD edge cases | — |
42	| `worlds` | Snake, the three fish-tank variants, predator-prey and the two check worlds, behind `worlds.make` | SNAKE-01–SNAKE-06, FISH-01–FISH-08, PREY-01–PREY-06, TRAIN-06 (check worlds) | `core` |
43	| `baselines` | Random play for any action format, each world's hand-written rule acting on observations only, and their mean scores on the evaluation seeds | EVAL-01, EVAL-02, SNAKE-07, FISH-09, PREY-07 | `core`, `worlds` |
44	| `methods` | Hand-written DQN and PPO learners behind `Learner`; rebuilds a frozen `Policy` from a checkpoint | CHECK-01, TRAIN-07 | `core`, `runlog` |
45	| `train` | One run over the step budget: per-team learners, checkpoints, in-training evaluations, invalid-number stop; batches across cores; the check-world gate | TRAIN-01–TRAIN-07, QR-01–QR-03, TRAIN edge cases | `core`, `worlds`, `baselines`, `methods`, `runlog` |
46	| `runlog` | Run folders: record, episode and evaluation logs, checkpoints, live progress, run lookup | LOG-01, LOG-02, LOG-06, TRAIN-03 (storage), TRAIN edge cases (overwrite, incomplete) | `core` |
47	| `charts` | Learning curves with range bands and baseline lines, extra measures, two-team curves, pairing heatmaps; follows the `dataviz` skill | LOG-03–LOG-05, PREY-10, PREY-09 (chart), LOG edge cases | `core`, `baselines`, `runlog`, `evaluate` |
48	| `evaluate` | Final evaluation on the final seeds, paired permutation tests run with the `null-hypothesis-testing` skill, verdicts, summaries, fixed-opponent team evaluation, pairing grids, collapse flags | EVAL-03–EVAL-06, SNAKE-08, FISH-10, PREY-08, PREY-09, QR-05, EVAL and PREY edge cases | `core`, `worlds`, `baselines`, `methods`, `runlog` |
49	| `replay` | Opens replay sides, re-simulates checkpoints or baselines into a `Trace`; pygame viewer with pause, step, speeds, side by side and sight overlay, testable headless; MP4 export | REPLAY-01–REPLAY-08, QR-04, REPLAY edge cases | `core`, `worlds`, `baselines`, `methods`, `runlog` |
50	| `refcheck` | Trains and evaluates Stable-Baselines3 DQN/PPO on a world through a Gymnasium/VecEnv adapter, and compares with the hand-written summary | CHECK-02, CHECK-03, CHECK edge case | `core`, `worlds`, `train`, `evaluate`, `runlog` |
51	| `notes` | Write-up scaffold with the three headings only, and the finish check (parts present, struggle link, broken links) | NOTES-01–NOTES-03, NOTES edge case | — |
52	| `cli` | The `rllab` command: dispatches each trigger to its pipeline entry and prints the result; owns no step (AD-10) | LOG-06 (view), REPLAY-01 (entry) | `core`, `worlds`, `baselines`, `methods`, `train`, `runlog`, `charts`, `evaluate`, `replay`, `refcheck`, `notes` |
