54	## Interfaces
55	See [packages/rllab/contract.md](packages/rllab/contract.md).
56	
57	## Data
58	No external datasets: every world is simulated. Generated data lives under `runs/<world>/<variant>/<run-name>/` (AD-06):
59	- `run.json`: the LOG-01 record, the run's config and its status (`running`, `finished`, `incomplete`, `failed` with step and value).
60	- `episodes.jsonl`: one line per training episode (LOG-02). `evals.jsonl`: one line per 5% evaluation point (TRAIN-04), flushed as written so `rllab watch` reads it live (LOG-06).
61	- `checkpoints/ckpt_<pct>.pt`: 11 checkpoints, every 10% (TRAIN-03), each holding the `WorldSpec`, per-team method configs and state dicts.
62	- Final evaluations under `runs/<world>/<variant>/_eval/<date>/` (`summary.json`, per-episode scores), reference runs under `_reference/`, charts and videos under `reports/<world>/`, write-ups at `notes/<world>.md`.
63	
64	Seeds come from three disjoint ranges (AD-05): training episode seeds drawn by the run's generator from [0, 10⁶), the 20 in-training evaluation seeds 1,000,000–1,000,019, and the 100 final seeds 2,000,000–2,000,099, shared by every run and baseline.
65	
66	## Cross-cutting concerns
67	- **Determinism (WORLD-02, TRAIN-02, REPLAY-02, FISH-11, PREY-11):** no global random state. Each episode seed spawns the world's and the policies' generators, and torch runs single-threaded with deterministic algorithms (AD-05). A committed digest of a short snake run's logs and final checkpoint is checked in CI. A later world that changes it fails the build (FISH-11, PREY-11), and so does a fish digest once S2 lands.
68	- **No outside RL code in training (CHECK-01):** import-linter forbids `stable_baselines3` and `gymnasium` outside `refcheck`, and a test asserts neither is in `sys.modules` after a training run (AD-08).
69	- **Speed (QR-01, QR-02, QR-04, QR-05):** world physics is NumPy over all agents at once, learners batch every team's observations into one forward pass, and evaluation spreads seeds over processes. `rllab` logs each run's wall time against the 60-minute budget, and a timing test plays a 1,000-step replay headless.
70	- **GPU (QR-03):** `device` is a run setting, `cpu` by default; a rented GPU run uses the same code, and its cost goes in the write-up by hand.
71	- **Offline (QR-06):** nothing imports a network client. ffmpeg comes bundled with imageio-ffmpeg, and fonts come from pygame's built-in one.
72	- **Outputs:** every step that writes a chart, video or write-up returns a `core.Output` naming the file and what it was made from, so a write-up can cite it (NOTES-02).
73	- **Errors:** bad actions raise `core.ActionError` naming the agent and value (never clipped). Spec mismatches on checkpoint load raise `methods.SpecMismatchError` naming the field. Mixed-settings charts raise `charts.MixedRunsError` naming the differing settings.
74	- **Skills:** `charts` follows `dataviz` for every chart (LOG-04). `evaluate.compare` is built and checked with `null-hypothesis-testing`, whose inputs are its signature (EVAL-04).
75	
76	## Project layout
77	```
78	src/rllab/     → core/ worlds/ baselines/ methods/ train/ runlog/ charts/ evaluate/ replay/ refcheck/ notes/ cli/ — one folder per component; __init__ exports its contract
79	                 train/pipelines/ holds the PL-01–PL-03 entries, cli/pipelines/ the PL-04–PL-12 entries (AD-10)
80	configs/       → one TOML per world and variant: budget, per-team method settings
81	runs/          → generated run folders (git-ignored)
82	reports/       → charts and videos kept alongside the write-ups
83	notes/         → one write-up per world, the learner's own words
84	tests/         → folders only; files are planned in pipelines.md
85	```
86	
87	## Decisions
88	### AD-01: Python 3.12 in one uv-managed package
89	**Status:** Accepted · **Date:** 2026-10-17 · **Scope:** system
90	- **Context:** One learner on an Apple Silicon laptop; PyTorch, pygame-ce and Stable-Baselines3 all ship arm64 macOS wheels for Python. QR-06 needs the whole install to work offline once set up.
91	- **Decision:** Python 3.12, one `rllab` package with a `uv.lock`. Stable-Baselines3 and Gymnasium go in an optional `check` extra.
92	- **Alternatives:** Python 3.13: wheels for some libraries lag. Separate `rllab-check` package: a real boundary only if the reference stack becomes a burden, so it is a named seam for now.
93	- **Consequences:** `requires-python = ">=3.12,<3.13"`. Training can run without the `check` extra installed, which proves CHECK-01 at install level too.
94	
95	### AD-02: PyTorch for networks and gradients, not for the learning algorithm
96	**Status:** Accepted · **Date:** 2026-10-17 · **Scope:** system
97	- **Context:** CHECK-01 allows automatic gradient computation but the learner writes action choice, learning targets and updates. Networks are small MLPs on observations of tens of numbers (WORLD-03). Training is CPU-only (QR-01).
98	- **Decision:** Learners use `torch.nn` layers, `torch.optim` optimisers and autograd. Replay buffers, exploration, targets, advantage estimation, clipping and update schedules are hand-written in `methods`.
99	- **Alternatives:** NumPy with hand-derived backprop: teaches backprop, which is not this project's subject, and is slower to debug. JAX: capable, but less familiar CPU tooling and a functional style that hides the update loop.
100	- **Consequences:** `torch.set_num_threads(1)` per run process (AD-05); small batches stay fast on CPU. The PyTorch install is about 200 MB.
101	
102	### AD-03: DQN for discrete actions, PPO for continuous ones, one learner per team
103	**Status:** Accepted · **Date:** 2026-10-17 · **Scope:** system
104	- **Context:** Snake has 3 discrete actions (SNAKE-02); fish and predator-prey push with two numbers in [−1, 1] (FISH-02, PREY-02). Many-fish has 8 agents (FISH-06), and predator-prey has two teams learning at once (TRAIN-07). The brief wants each world to teach one idea, and wants checking against a reference implementation (CHECK-02).
105	- **Decision:** `dqn` (replay buffer, target network, ε-greedy, Huber loss) for snake and the discrete check world. `ppo` (Gaussian policy, GAE, clipped objective) for fish, predator-prey and the continuous check world. All agents of a team share one learner (parameter sharing over a batch of agents). Teams have separate learners in the same run. Evaluation and replay act deterministically: greedy for DQN, the mean action for PPO.
106	- **Alternatives:** PPO everywhere: one method fewer, but snake would not teach value learning. SAC for fish: strong on continuous control, but a third method and harder to explain for many agents. Tabular Q-learning for snake: SNAKE-06 includes length, an unbounded number, so the table would need discretising.
107	- **Consequences:** Two methods to write, each with a check world (TRAIN-06) and a Stable-Baselines3 equivalent (AD-08). A prey that leaves the episode is masked out of its team's batch.
108	
109	### AD-04: Our own multi-agent World protocol, not Gymnasium or PettingZoo
110	**Status:** Accepted · **Date:** 2026-10-17 · **Scope:** system
111	- **Context:** WORLD-01–WORLD-07 need seeded resets, a declared spec, team-labelled agents, per-team scores with a direction, and a drawable state. The brief bets that one core serves a grid game, smooth movement and two teams.
112	- **Decision:** `core.World` is a small protocol. Observations, actions, rewards and active masks are NumPy arrays indexed by agent, teams map to agent indices in `WorldSpec`, and `draw()` returns a `DrawState`. "Not seen" entries are a 0/1 flag followed by zeros, so lengths stay fixed (WORLD-03, FISH-07, PREY-06).
113	- **Alternatives:** Gymnasium: single-agent, with no teams, score direction or drawable state, so all of those would be extras. PettingZoo parallel API: multi-agent, but dict-per-agent stepping is slow in Python and adds a dependency the learner didn't write.
114	- **Consequences:** `refcheck` writes the one Gymnasium/VecEnv adapter (AD-08). Every world implements all five methods, and `core.play_episode` is the only episode loop.
115	
116	### AD-05: Seeds from SeedSequence, disjoint held-out ranges, one process per run
117	**Status:** Accepted · **Date:** 2026-10-17 · **Scope:** system
118	- **Context:** WORLD-01, WORLD-02, TRAIN-02 and REPLAY-02 need exact repeats; TRAIN-04 and EVAL-03 need seeds never used in training; TRAIN-05 and QR-02 need runs in parallel, one per core.
119	- **Decision:** A run seed spawns the learner's, exploration and episode-seed generators through `np.random.SeedSequence`. An episode seed spawns the world's and the policies' generators inside `core.play_episode`. Training draws episode seeds from [0, 10⁶); evaluation seeds are the fixed `core.EVAL_SEEDS` and `core.FINAL_SEEDS` ranges (see Data). `train.run_batch` runs each config in its own `spawn` process with `torch.set_num_threads(1)` and `torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True)`.
120	- **Alternatives:** Global `np.random.seed`/`torch.manual_seed`: order-dependent, breaks once evaluation interleaves with training. Threads: the GIL serialises the Python world loop.
121	- **Consequences:** Identical runs are promised on the same machine only (TRAIN-02). In-training evaluation never touches the training generators, so turning evaluation off for a test leaves training identical.
122	
123	### AD-06: Run folders of JSON, JSON Lines and torch checkpoints
124	**Status:** Accepted · **Date:** 2026-10-17 · **Scope:** system
125	- **Context:** LOG-01 and LOG-02 need a record and per-episode logs. LOG-06 needs them readable while the run writes, at most one evaluation point behind. A stopped run keeps its checkpoints and is marked incomplete. A run name that already exists is refused (TRAIN edge cases). REPLAY edge cases need a checkpoint to carry its world spec.
126	- **Decision:** One folder per run (see Data). Logs are append-only JSON Lines, flushed at every evaluation point; `run.json` is rewritten atomically (write, then rename) on each status change. A checkpoint is a `torch.save` dict of spec, method configs and state dicts, loaded with `weights_only=True`.
127	- **Alternatives:** Parquet: fast to read but not appendable while a run is live. SQLite: one file for everything, but concurrent writers from parallel runs need care and the files are no longer readable by eye.
128	- **Consequences:** Charts and evaluation read whole JSONL files; at ~10⁴ episodes per run that is instant. A crashed process leaves `run.json` at `running`; `runlog` reports any `running` run with no live process as incomplete.
129	
130	### AD-07: Replays re-simulate into a Trace, shown with pygame-ce and saved with imageio-ffmpeg
131	**Status:** Accepted · **Date:** 2026-10-17 · **Scope:** system
132	- **Context:** REPLAY-01–REPLAY-08 need any checkpoint or baseline from any seed, step back, 4 speeds, side by side, a sight overlay and video export. QR-04 needs ≥ 30 steps/s; QR-06 needs it offline. Worlds are deterministic (AD-05), so nothing needs recording during training.
133	- **Decision:** `replay.trace_sides` (or, for one side, `to_trace` over a traced `core.play_episode`) plays the whole episode headless through `core.play_episode` into a `Trace` of frames (at most the world's `max_steps`), then the viewer only draws frames. Stepping back is an index change. 1× is 30 steps/s. pygame-ce draws circles, cells and text; MP4 (H.264) export goes through imageio's ffmpeg writer.
134	- **Alternatives:** matplotlib animation: slow at 16× for 8 fish and clumsy keyboard control. A browser viewer: smooth, but a local web server and JavaScript stack for one user (spec: everything local).
135	- **Consequences:** The `Trace` is testable without a window, so REPLAY-02 is a frame comparison. Memory is one episode of `DrawState`s, under 1,000 frames of a few dozen objects.
136	
137	### AD-08: Stable-Baselines3 as the reference, isolated in `refcheck`
138	**Status:** Accepted · **Date:** 2026-10-17 · **Scope:** system
139	- **Context:** CHECK-02 needs an established implementation of the same kind of method (DQN, PPO; AD-03) trained on the same world, budget and seeds; CHECK-01 forbids it inside training runs. Worlds speak `core.World`, not Gymnasium (AD-04).
140	- **Decision:** `refcheck` wraps a world as a Gymnasium env (single agent) or as an SB3 `VecEnv` with one slot per agent of the learning team (many-fish: 8 slots stepping one world, matching parameter sharing). It trains SB3 `DQN` or `PPO` for 5 seeds and evaluates on `core.FINAL_SEEDS` through `core.play_episode`. In predator-prey, SB3 cannot co-train two models, so each team's reference trains against the rule-based opponent, the same opponent PREY-08 evaluates against.
141	- **Alternatives:** CleanRL single-file scripts: easy to read, but they would need editing to fit our worlds, which weakens them as a reference. RLlib: multi-agent native, but a heavy distributed stack for a laptop.
142	- **Consequences:** SB3's default hyperparameters differ from ours; `refcheck` starts from the hand-written method's settings where SB3 has the same knob. The predator-prey reference answers a narrower question than co-training, so CHECK-03's 80% bar compares a fixed-opponent reference with a co-trained team there; the learner accepted this (2026-10-17).
143	
144	### AD-09: Every batch first runs the method's check world
145	**Status:** Accepted · **Date:** 2026-10-17 · **Scope:** system
146	- **Context:** TRAIN-06 needs every learning method to learn a hand-derived best behaviour before it is used in a world. Bugs in RL are silent (brief), and methods change while a world is built.
147	- **Decision:** `train.run_check` trains each team method in a config on its check world: for DQN a 6-state corridor with two exits, left of state 0 (reward 0.8) and right of state 5 (reward 1.0), γ = 0.9, so the best action is left in states 0–1 and right in 2–5 and a learner biased to one action can't pass; for PPO a 1-D point that is rewarded for reaching a target, where the best push is the sign of the offset. It needs ≥ 95% agreement over every check-world state. `train.run_batch` runs the same check steps with the batch's method settings before starting any run and refuses the batch on failure. `rllab check <config>` runs it alone.
148	- **Alternatives:** A recorded pass per method version: saves seconds but goes stale silently when code changes. A test only: passes in CI but not for the settings actually used.
149	- **Consequences:** Each batch spends under a minute on the check. A check-world settings override exists because a world's tuned settings (e.g. buffer size) may be too large for a 6-state world.
150	
151	### AD-10: Pipeline entries are the public API; `cli` only dispatches
152	**Status:** Accepted · **Date:** 2026-10-17 · **Scope:** system
153	- **Context:** Pipeline design gave every workflow one entry function calling named steps (PL-01–PL-12). Entries import their steps' owners, so where an entry lives sets an import direction. PL-03 runs PL-02 in worker processes, so `train` can't depend on `cli`. A chart, a summary and a heatmap each need two or more components that the `cli` row already depends on.
154	- **Decision:** `train` hosts the PL-01–PL-03 entries (`run_check`, `run_train`, `run_batch`) and exports them as its contract. Every other entry lives in `cli/pipelines/`. Entries return contract types. `cli/__main__.py` parses arguments, loads configs through `train.load_run_configs`, calls the entry, prints its result and sets the exit code; `cli` owns no pipeline step.
155	- **Alternatives:** All entries in `cli`: the batch's worker processes would import `cli`, inverting the dependency. Each entry in its main component: `charts` and `evaluate` entries would need `runlog` and `baselines` steps, which they may do, but the replay and refcheck entries would need printing in their own components.
156	- **Consequences:** The contract carries the train entries, the config loader and every step function a `cli` entry calls. `cli` has no step tests; its intent tests drive `main` with arguments.
157	
158	### AD-11: Stay a single package past the size triggers
159	**Status:** Accepted · **Date:** 2026-10-17 · **Scope:** system
160	- **Context:** The contract passed its 200-line size ceiling and the package has 12 pipelines, past the ~3-pipeline Grow trigger. The learner chose to stay single (2026-10-17). Nothing runs or deploys on its own, and the one dependency stack the rest shouldn't carry (Stable-Baselines3, Gymnasium) is already isolated as the optional `check` extra in `refcheck` (AD-08).
161	- **Decision:** `rllab` stays one package. Size is handled by `dt context`, which loads only the contract sections and pipelines a task reads, and import-linter keeps the component directions.
162	- **Alternatives:** Split `refcheck` into its own package: a real boundary, but already met by the extra. Split the shared core from the worlds: every world needs the core, and the core has no other consumer.
163	- **Consequences:** The Grow triggers stay reported by `dt` and are answered by this decision. A new trigger (a part that runs on its own, a heavy new stack) reopens it.
164	
165	### AD-12: Run and method settings types live in `core`
166	**Status:** Accepted · **Date:** 2026-10-18 · **Scope:** system
167	- **Context:** The M1 interface task found that `runlog.RunRecord`, `runlog.RunWriter` and `runlog.Checkpoint` hold a run's config and per-team method configs (LOG-01, AD-06), whose types `train` and `methods` owned. `runlog` may import only `core`, and import-linter counts `TYPE_CHECKING` imports too, so no version of those types could be written.
168	- **Decision:** A type that a lower component stores but a higher one fills lives in `core`. `RunConfig`, `MethodConfig` and the `Settings` alias are `core` types. `methods` still declares each method's keys and defaults, and `train.load_run_configs` still builds and fills the configs; neither owns the types.
169	- **Alternatives:** Move `RunRecord`, `Checkpoint` and `RunWriter` up into `train`: `methods`, `evaluate`, `replay` and `charts` read checkpoints and records, so they would all depend on `train`, and the batch's worker processes would import them. Store the config in `runlog` as an untyped mapping: loses the frozen type at every reader.
170	- **Consequences:** `runlog` keeps `core` as its only dependency, and the import layers are unchanged. Every consumer imports `RunConfig` and `MethodConfig` from `core`.
171	
172	## Risks and open questions
173	- **Many-fish and predator-prey speed (QR-01, QR-02).** 8–10 agents, 1,000-step episodes and 21 × 20 evaluation episodes may not fit 60 minutes in Python. Mitigation: NumPy physics over all agents, evaluation in a worker process, and a timing check in the first fish build; QR-03's GPU route doesn't help a CPU-bound world loop.
174	- **5 parallel runs on mixed cores (QR-02).** Apple Silicon's efficiency cores run slower; 5 runs may not all land on performance cores. Measured in the first snake batch.
175

224	- **Invariants:** loads one config per seed, runs the method check with `check_seed` before any run, then one process per config up to `workers` (default: the core count), each with one torch thread (TRAIN-05, TRAIN-06, AD-05)

326	- **Invariants:** `difference` is signed so positive means better in the team's direction

330	### evaluate.compare: Paired permutation test
331	`def compare(learned: Array, baseline: Array, direction: Direction, *, permutations: int = 10_000, rng: np.random.Generator) -> tuple[float, float]`
332	- **Invariants:** `learned` is runs × seeds, averaged over runs per seed; `baseline` has one score per seed, on the same seeds; swaps labels within each seed; returns (difference, one-sided p) as EVAL-04 states; built and checked with the `null-hypothesis-testing` skill

336	### evaluate.Summary: type
337	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Summary: world: str; variant: str; team: str; runs: tuple[str, ...]; learned_mean: float; learned_range: tuple[float, float]; run_means: Mapping[str, float]; random_mean: float; rule_mean: float; comparisons: tuple[Comparison, ...]; verdict: Verdict; collapse: int | None`
338	`Verdict(beats_baselines: bool, failing_runs: tuple[str, ...], runs_needed: int)`
339	- **Invariants:** one per world, variant and team (EVAL-06, PREY-08); `comparisons` empty and `runs_needed > 0` with fewer than 5 runs; `collapse` is set when the team's in-training eval score stays at or below its random-baseline mean for the last 4+ consecutive eval points, and is the step of the first of them
340	- **Boundary:** charts, refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.Summary/ · **Cases:** a passing snake summary; one with a failing run; one with 3 runs
341	- **Used by:** refcheck, cli; PL-06

343	### evaluate.final_evaluation: Final evaluation
344	`def final_evaluation(runs: Sequence[Run], out: Path, *, workers: int | None = None) -> list[Summary]`
345	- **Invariants:** plays every final checkpoint and both baselines on `FINAL_SEEDS` (EVAL-03); Holm across the 2 comparisons per team; a pass needs both nulls rejected (EVAL-04) and every run's mean beating the rule mean (EVAL-05); in predator-prey each team plays against the rule-based opponent (PREY-08); within 10 minutes (QR-05)
346	- **Boundary:** refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.final_evaluation/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the passing snake summary
347	- **Used by:** refcheck, cli; PL-06

349	### evaluate.load_summaries: Saved summaries
350	`def load_summaries(root: Path, world: str, variant: str) -> list[Summary]`
351	- **Invariants:** the latest `_eval/<date>/summary.json` for that world and variant, one `Summary` per team
352	- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` naming `rllab evaluate` when none exists
353	- **Boundary:** refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.load_summaries/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the passing snake summary
354	- **Used by:** refcheck; PL-09
355	
356	### evaluate.PairingGrid: type
357	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class PairingGrid: run: str; prey_pcts: tuple[int, ...]; predator_pcts: tuple[int, ...]; scores: Mapping[str, Array]`
358	- **Invariants:** `scores` keyed by team, each 11 × 11 (one row and column per checkpoint), cell [i, j] the mean of 20 `EVAL_SEEDS` episodes of prey checkpoint i against predator checkpoint j (PREY-09)
359	- **Boundary:** charts · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.PairingGrid/ · **Cases:** an 11 × 11 grid with planted values
360	- **Used by:** charts; PL-12
361	
362	### evaluate.pairing_grid: Checkpoint pairings
363	`def pairing_grid(run: Run, *, workers: int | None = None) -> PairingGrid`
364	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the run's world doesn't have exactly two teams
365	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.pairing_grid/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the planted 11 × 11 grid
366	- **Used by:** cli; PL-12
367	
368	## replay
369	
370	### replay.Trace: type
371	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Trace: label: str; spec: WorldSpec; seed: int; frames: tuple[Frame, ...]; hit_step_limit: bool`
372	- **Invariants:** same sources and seed → equal frames (REPLAY-02); `label` names the checkpoint or baseline per team
373	- **Boundary:** replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.Trace/ · **Cases:** the rule snake on seed 7
374	- **Used by:** replay.view, replay.save_video; PL-07, PL-08
375	
376	### replay.open_sides: Replay sides
377	`def open_sides(world: str, variant: str, sides: Sequence[str]) -> list[Mapping[str, Checkpoint | Literal["random", "rule"]]]`
378	- **Invariants:** one or two sides, each `<run-path>@<pct>`, `random` or `rule`; predator-prey sides may name each team (`prey=<run>@<pct>,predator=<run>@<pct>`); checkpoints read through `runlog.open_run`
379	- **Errors:** `ValueError` naming a malformed side or a third side; `SpecMismatchError` naming each differing field when a checkpoint's spec no longer matches the world (REPLAY edge case)
380	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.open_sides/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the `rule` side and a 100% snake checkpoint side
381	- **Used by:** cli; PL-07
382	
383	### replay.open_sources: One replay side
384	`def open_sources(world: str, variant: str, side: str) -> Mapping[str, Checkpoint | Literal["random", "rule"]]`
385	- **Invariants:** one side in `open_sides`'s format
386	- **Errors:** as `open_sides`
387	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.open_sources/ · **Cases:** a fake returning a 50% snake checkpoint side
388	- **Used by:** cli; PL-08
389	
390	### replay.policies_for: Side to policies
391	`def policies_for(sources: Mapping[str, Checkpoint | Literal["random", "rule"]], spec: WorldSpec) -> Mapping[str, Policy]`
392	- **Invariants:** one policy per team of `spec`; a team left out takes its policy from the first checkpoint given, so one predator-prey checkpoint replays both teams (REPLAY-01, REPLAY-06, PREY-09)
393	- **Errors:** `SpecMismatchError` from `methods.load_policy`
394	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.policies_for/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the snake rule policy
395	- **Used by:** cli; PL-08
396	
397	### replay.to_trace: Episode to trace
398	`def to_trace(episode: Episode, spec: WorldSpec, label: str) -> Trace`
399	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `episode.frames` is `None`
400	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.to_trace/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the rule-snake seed-7 trace
401	- **Used by:** cli, replay.trace_sides; PL-08
402	
403	### replay.trace_sides: Traces for each side
404	`def trace_sides(world: str, variant: str, sides: Sequence[Mapping[str, Checkpoint | Literal["random", "rule"]]], seed: int) -> list[Trace]`
405	- **Invariants:** per side, a fresh world, `policies_for`, `core.play_episode` (traced) and `to_trace`; every side starts from the same seed (REPLAY-02, REPLAY-04)
406	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.trace_sides/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the rule snake and random snake traces on seed 7
407	- **Used by:** cli; PL-07
408	
409	### replay.Session: type
410	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Session: labels: tuple[str, ...]; last_step: tuple[int, ...]; speed: float; overlay_sight: bool`
411	- **Invariants:** one entry per trace shown; `last_step` is the frame each trace showed when the window closed; `speed` in {0.25, 1, 4, 16}
412	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.Session/ · **Cases:** one trace closed at step 30; two traces with one ended early
413	- **Used by:** cli; PL-07
414	
415	### replay.view: Replay window
416	`def view(traces: Sequence[Trace], *, overlay_sight: bool = False, keys: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> Session`
417	- **Invariants:** one or two traces side by side, stepping in sync, a finished trace holding its last frame (REPLAY-04); pause, step ±1 and 0.25×/1×/4×/16× where 1× is 30 steps/s (REPLAY-03, QR-04); step, scores and rewards shown (REPLAY-05); a sight toggle when `spec.sight` is set (REPLAY-08); "stopped at step limit" shown when `hit_step_limit`; with `keys`, plays those key presses in a hidden window and closes, so it runs headless in tests
418	- **Errors:** `ValueError` for more than two traces
419	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.view/ · **Cases:** a fake returning a `Session` for the traces and overlay flag it was given
420	- **Used by:** cli; PL-07
421	
422	### replay.save_video: Replay to MP4
423	`def save_video(trace: Trace, out: Path, *, overlay_sight: bool = False) -> Output`
424	- **Invariants:** the whole episode at 1× (30 frames/s) as H.264 MP4 that plays in a standard player (REPLAY-07); offline (QR-06)
425	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.save_video/ · **Cases:** a fake writing an empty file at `out`
426	- **Used by:** cli; PL-08
427	
428	## refcheck
429	
430	### refcheck.CheckReport: type
431	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class CheckReport: world: str; variant: str; team: str; reference_mean: float; reference_range: tuple[float, float]; hand_mean: float; hand_range: tuple[float, float]; ratio: float; outcome: Literal["pass", "investigate-low", "investigate-high"]`
432	- **Invariants:** `ratio` is hand ÷ reference; for higher-is-better a pass is 0.8 ≤ ratio ≤ 1.5, and for lower-is-better 2/3 ≤ ratio ≤ 1.25 (CHECK-03, CHECK edge case)
433	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/refcheck.CheckReport/ · **Cases:** 18 vs 20 food (pass); 15 vs 20 (investigate-low)
434	- **Used by:** cli; PL-09
435	
436	### refcheck.reference_check: Reference implementation check
437	`def reference_check(configs: Sequence[RunConfig], root: Path) -> list[CheckReport]`
438	- **Invariants:** trains SB3 `DQN` or `PPO` for each config (same world, budget and seeds as the hand-written runs; AD-08) under `_reference/`, evaluates on `FINAL_SEEDS` (CHECK-02), and compares with `evaluate.load_summaries`; one report per team; the only code importing `stable_baselines3` or `gymnasium`
439	- **Errors:** `ImportError` naming the `check` extra when it isn't installed; `FileNotFoundError` from `load_summaries` when the hand-written runs have no final evaluation
440	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/refcheck.reference_check/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the 18-vs-20 passing report
441	- **Used by:** cli; PL-09
442	
443	## notes
444	
445	### notes.scaffold: New write-up
446	`def scaffold(world: str, root: Path) -> Output`
447	- **Invariants:** writes `notes/<world>.md` with only the three part headings (NOTES-01, NOTES-03)
448	- **Errors:** `FileExistsError` if the write-up exists; never overwrites
449	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/notes.scaffold/ · **Cases:** a fake writing the three-heading write-up
450	- **Used by:** cli; PL-10
451	
452	### notes.FinishReport: type
453	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class FinishReport: world: str; empty_parts: tuple[str, ...]; struggle_links: tuple[str, ...]; broken_links: tuple[str, ...]; ok: bool`
454	- **Invariants:** `ok` only when no part is empty, the struggle part links at least one existing file under `reports/` (NOTES-02) and no link is broken (NOTES edge case)
455	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/notes.FinishReport/ · **Cases:** a complete write-up; one with a broken chart link
456	- **Used by:** cli; PL-11
457	
458	### notes.finish_check: World finish check
459	`def finish_check(world: str, root: Path) -> FinishReport`
460	- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` if the write-up doesn't exist
461	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/notes.finish_check/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the broken-link report
462	- **Used by:** cli; PL-11
463	
464	## cli
465	
466	### cli.main: The rllab command
467	`def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int`
468	- **Invariants:** subcommands `check`, `train`, `batch`, `watch`, `chart`, `evaluate`, `grid`, `refcheck`, `replay`, `video`, `notes new`, `notes check`, each dispatched to its pipeline entry (`train` is a one-seed `batch`); prints the entry's returned contract type; owns no pipeline step (AD-10); every error is printed as one line naming its cause, exit code 1
469	- **Used by:** the learner

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Floor checks on the diff (CONSTRAINTS.md Floor, CR-01 to CR-04, CR-07, CR-08): new suppression
# comments, stubs, weakened or assertion-free tests, secrets, a loosened tool config, and
# imports from references/ or probes/, or either folder put on an import path.
# Diff-scoped, so code that was already there is not judged again.
#
#   scripts/floor_guard.sh                default base: refs/worktree/checks-base, else HEAD
#   scripts/floor_guard.sh --base REF     e.g. the milestone's Base sha, or origin/main in CI
#   scripts/floor_guard.sh --exit         the milestone exit: also fail on any interface stub left
#
# Interface stubs (`raise NotImplementedError("M1-T05")`) are judged over the whole project, not the
# diff: each must name a task still open in tasks/todo.md (CR-02).
#
# Exit 0 clean · 1 at least one violation · 2 could not run (never read a 2 as clean).
# Adapted from agent-skills constraint-driven-development references/floor-guard.md.
set -u
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." || exit 2

base_ref=""
exit_flag=()
while [ $# -gt 0 ]; do
  case "$1" in
    --base) base_ref="${2:-}"; shift 2 || shift ;;
    --exit) exit_flag=(--exit); shift ;;
    *) echo "usage: scripts/floor_guard.sh [--base REF] [--exit]" >&2; exit 2 ;;
  esac
done

if ! git rev-parse --is-inside-work-tree >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  echo "floor_guard: not inside a git work tree" >&2
  exit 2
fi
if [ -n "$base_ref" ]; then
  base=$(git rev-parse -q --verify "${base_ref}^{commit}") || {
    echo "floor_guard: no such commit: $base_ref" >&2
    exit 2
  }
else
  base=$(git rev-parse -q --verify refs/worktree/checks-base || git rev-parse -q --verify HEAD) || {
    echo "floor_guard: no commit to compare against: commit first" >&2
    exit 2
  }
fi

tmp=$(mktemp -d) || exit 2
trap 'rm -rf "$tmp"' EXIT
# The project may sit below the git root: --relative limits the diff to it and names files
# relative to it, as `git ls-files` does from here.
git -c core.quotepath=off diff --relative --unified=0 --no-color --no-ext-diff "$base" -- . >"$tmp/diff" || exit 2
git -c core.quotepath=off ls-files --others --exclude-standard -- . >"$tmp/untracked" || exit 2
git show "$base:./pyproject.toml" >"$tmp/pyproject.base.toml" 2>/dev/null || : >"$tmp/pyproject.base.toml"
git -c core.quotepath=off ls-files --cached --others --exclude-standard -- '*.py' >"$tmp/python" || exit 2

uv run --frozen --quiet python scripts/floor_guard.py \
  --diff "$tmp/diff" --untracked "$tmp/untracked" --old-pyproject "$tmp/pyproject.base.toml" \
  --python "$tmp/python" ${exit_flag[@]+"${exit_flag[@]}"}
status=$?
# Anything but a clean 0 or a findings 1 means the guard itself broke.
[ $status -le 1 ] || status=2
exit $status

1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Diff-scoped floor checks for CONSTRAINTS.md (CR-01 to CR-04, CR-07, CR-08).
3	
4	Called by scripts/floor_guard.sh, which writes the diff, the untracked-file list and the
5	base commit's pyproject.toml. Prints one line per finding and exits 1 if there are any:
6	
7	    path:line: [rule] message
8	
9	Rules (CONSTRAINTS.md ID in brackets):
10	    suppression [CR-01]            a new `type: ignore`, `pyright:`, `noqa`, `pragma: no cover`...
11	    stub [CR-02]                   a function whose body is only pass, ... or NotImplementedError
12	    stub-task [CR-02]              an interface stub, `raise NotImplementedError("M1-T05")`, naming
13	                                   a task that is not open in tasks/todo.md (whole repo, not the diff)
14	    stub-left [CR-02]              any interface stub, with --exit (the milestone exit)
15	    swallowed-exception [CR-02]    `except:` / `except Exception:` with only pass
16	    test-skipped [CR-03]           a new skip, skipif, xfail or importorskip
17	    test-deleted [CR-03]           a test file removed
18	    assertion-removed [CR-03]      a test file lost more assertion lines than it gained
19	    test-without-assertion [CR-03] a changed test function that asserts nothing
20	    secret [CR-04]                 a credential pattern (the value is never printed)
21	    references-import [CR-08]      an import from the references folder
22	    references-path [CR-08]        the references folder put on an import path (sys.path,
23	                                   an environment variable, pytest or type-checker config)
24	    probes-import, probes-path     the same for the probes folder [CR-08]
25	    bar-loosened [CR-07]           pyproject.toml tool config weakened, or a new config file
26	    rule-removed [CR-07]           a CR rule gone from CONSTRAINTS.md
27	    new-exception [CR-07]          a new row in CONSTRAINTS.md's Exceptions table
28	
29	An interface stub is the one placeholder body CR-02 allows: the milestone's interface task writes
30	each step signature that way, naming the task that replaces it, and testers import it before the
31	code exists. It is never a `stub` finding; `stub-task` and `stub-left` judge it instead.
32	
33	A CR-01 to CR-03 or CR-08 finding is allowed when an unexpired row in CONSTRAINTS.md's
34	Exceptions table names that rule and a path glob matching the file. Secrets and CR-07 never are.
35	Files under scripts/ are skipped for code checks: they are the bar, protected separately.
36	Files under references/ and probes/ are inputs, not project code: only the secret check reads them.
37	"""

186	def is_test_file(path: str) -> bool:
187	    parts = Path(path).parts
188	    name = parts[-1] if parts else ""
189	    return (
190	        "tests" in parts
191	        and (name.startswith("test_") or name.endswith("_test.py"))
192	        and not is_outside(path)
193	    )

196	def line_findings(diff: Diff) -> list[Finding]:
197	    found: list[Finding] = []
198	    for path, lines in diff.added.items():
199	        python, test = is_python(path), is_test_file(path)
200	        path_settings = not is_outside(path) and not path.endswith(DOC_SUFFIXES)
201	        for n, text in lines:
202	            if python and SUPPRESSION.search(text):
203	                found.append(Finding("suppression", path, n, "new suppression comment"))
204	            if path_settings and IMPORT_PATH_SETTING.search(text):
205	                found += [
206	                    Finding(f"{folder}-path", path, n, f"{folder}/ on an import path")
207	                    for folder, word in OUTSIDE.items()
208	                    if word.search(text)
209	                ]
210	            if test and SKIP.search(text):
211	                found.append(Finding("test-skipped", path, n, "test skipped or marked xfail"))
212	            if Path(path).name not in LOCKFILES:
213	                found += [
214	                    Finding("secret", path, n, f"looks like a {kind} (value not shown)")
215	                    for kind, pattern in SECRETS.items()
216	                    if pattern.search(text)
217	                ]
218	    return found

220	
221	def test_file_findings(diff: Diff) -> list[Finding]:
222	    found = [
223	        Finding("test-deleted", p, 0, "test file deleted") for p in diff.deleted if is_test_file(p)
224	    ]
225	    for path, lines in diff.removed.items():
226	        if not is_test_file(path) or path in diff.deleted:
227	            continue
228	        lost = sum(bool(ASSERTION.search(t)) for t in lines)
229	        gained = sum(bool(ASSERTION.search(t)) for _, t in diff.added.get(path, []))
230	        if lost > gained:
231	            found.append(
232	                Finding("assertion-removed", path, 0, f"{lost - gained} assertion line(s) removed")
233	            )
234	    return found

238	def _touches(node: ast.AST, lines: set[int]) -> bool:
239	    start = getattr(node, "lineno", 0)
240	    end = getattr(node, "end_lineno", start) or start
241	    return any(start <= n <= end for n in lines)

244	def _is_trivial(body: list[ast.stmt]) -> bool:
245	    """Only pass, `...` or `raise NotImplementedError`, after an optional docstring."""
246	    stmts = body[1:] if body and _is_docstring(body[0]) else body
247	    return all(_is_placeholder(s) for s in stmts)
248	
249	
250	def _task_stub(body: list[ast.stmt]) -> str | None:
251	    """The task an interface stub names: a body that is only `raise NotImplementedError("M1-T05")`."""
252	    stmts = body[1:] if body and _is_docstring(body[0]) else body
253	    if len(stmts) != 1 or not isinstance(stmts[0], ast.Raise):
254	        return None
255	    exc = stmts[0].exc
256	    if not (isinstance(exc, ast.Call) and _name(exc.func) == "NotImplementedError"):
257	        return None
258	    arg = exc.args[0] if len(exc.args) == 1 and not exc.keywords else None
259	    if (
260	        isinstance(arg, ast.Constant)
261	        and isinstance(arg.value, str)
262	        and TASK_ID.fullmatch(arg.value)
263	    ):
264	        return arg.value
265	    return None
266	
267	
268	def _is_docstring(stmt: ast.stmt) -> bool:
269	    return (
270	        isinstance(stmt, ast.Expr)
271	        and isinstance(stmt.value, ast.Constant)
272	        and isinstance(stmt.value.value, str)
273	    )
274	
275	
276	def _is_placeholder(stmt: ast.stmt) -> bool:
277	    if isinstance(stmt, ast.Pass):
278	        return True
279	    if isinstance(stmt, ast.Expr) and isinstance(stmt.value, ast.Constant):
280	        return stmt.value.value is Ellipsis
281	    if isinstance(stmt, ast.Raise) and stmt.exc is not None:
282	        exc = stmt.exc.func if isinstance(stmt.exc, ast.Call) else stmt.exc
283	        return _name(exc) == "NotImplementedError"
284	    return False
285	
286	
287	def _name(node: ast.AST) -> str | None:
288	    if isinstance(node, ast.Name):
289	        return node.id
290	    if isinstance(node, ast.Attribute):
291	        return node.attr
292	    if isinstance(node, ast.Call):
293	        return _name(node.func)
294	    return None
295	
296	
297	def _exempt_functions(tree: ast.Module) -> set[ast.AST]:
298	    """Abstract methods, overloads and Protocol members may have placeholder bodies."""
299	    exempt: set[ast.AST] = set()
300	    for node in ast.walk(tree):
301	        if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
302	            if {_name(d) for d in node.decorator_list} & EXEMPT_DECORATORS:
303	                exempt.add(node)
304	        elif isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef) and "Protocol" in {_name(b) for b in node.bases}:
305	            exempt.update(
306	                n for n in node.body if isinstance(n, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef))
307	            )
308	    return exempt
309	
310	
311	def _in_folder(name: str | None, folder: str) -> bool:
312	    return name is not None and (name == folder or name.startswith(f"{folder}."))
313	
314	
315	def _mentions(node: ast.AST, word: re.Pattern[str]) -> bool:
316	    return any(
317	        isinstance(n, ast.Constant) and isinstance(n.value, str) and word.search(n.value)
318	        for n in ast.walk(node)
319	    )
320	
321	
322	def _is_sys_path(node: ast.AST) -> bool:
323	    return (
324	        isinstance(node, ast.Attribute)
325	        and node.attr == "path"
326	        and isinstance(node.value, ast.Name)
327	        and node.value.id == "sys"
328	    )
329	
330	
331	def _imported_modules(node: ast.AST) -> list[str | None]:
332	    """Absolute module names an import statement or an import_module call brings in."""
333	    if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
334	        return [alias.name for alias in node.names]
335	    if isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):
336	        return [node.module] if node.level == 0 else []
337	    if isinstance(node, ast.Call) and _name(node.func) in {"import_module", "__import__"}:
338	        first = node.args[0] if node.args else None
339	        if isinstance(first, ast.Constant) and isinstance(first.value, str):
340	            return [first.value]
341	    return []
342	
343	
344	def _changes_sys_path(node: ast.AST) -> bool:
345	    """sys.path.insert/append/extend, site.addsitedir, or an assignment to sys.path."""
346	    if isinstance(node, ast.Call) and isinstance(node.func, ast.Attribute):
347	        func = node.func
348	        return func.attr == "addsitedir" or (
349	            func.attr in SYS_PATH_CALLS and _is_sys_path(func.value)
350	        )
351	    if isinstance(node, ast.Assign):
352	        return any(_is_sys_path(target) for target in node.targets)
353	    if isinstance(node, ast.AugAssign):
354	        return _is_sys_path(node.target)
355	    return False
356	
357	
358	def _outside_finding(path: str, node: ast.AST) -> Finding | None:
359	    """CR-08: an import from references/ or probes/, or either folder added to sys.path."""
360	    line = getattr(node, "lineno", 0)
361	    for folder, word in OUTSIDE.items():
362	        if any(_in_folder(name, folder) for name in _imported_modules(node)):
363	            return Finding(f"{folder}-import", path, line, f"import from {folder}/")
364	        if _changes_sys_path(node) and _mentions(node, word):
365	            return Finding(f"{folder}-path", path, line, f"{folder}/ added to sys.path")
366	    return None
367	
368	
369	def _asserts(func: ast.AST) -> bool:
370	    for node in ast.walk(func):
371	        if isinstance(node, ast.Assert):
372	            return True
373	        name = _name(node) if isinstance(node, ast.Call) else None
374	        if name and (
375	            name.startswith("assert") or name in {"raises", "warns", "deprecated_call", "fail"}
376	        ):
377	            return True
378	    return False
379	
380	
381	def ast_findings(path: str, lines: set[int]) -> list[Finding]:
382	    try:
383	        tree = ast.parse(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
384	    except (SyntaxError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):
385	        return []  # ruff reports syntax errors; a deleted file has nothing to check
386	    exempt, test = _exempt_functions(tree), is_test_file(path)
387	    found: list[Finding] = []
388	    for node in ast.walk(tree):
389	        if not _touches(node, lines):
390	            continue
391	        if outside := _outside_finding(path, node):
392	            found.append(outside)
393	        if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
394	            if (
395	                node not in exempt
396	                and not path.endswith(".pyi")
397	                and _is_trivial(node.body)
398	                and not _task_stub(node.body)
399	            ):
400	                found.append(
401	                    Finding("stub", path, node.lineno, f"`{node.name}` has a placeholder body")
402	                )
403	            if test and node.name.startswith("test") and not _asserts(node):
404	                found.append(
405	                    Finding(
406	                        "test-without-assertion",
407	                        path,
408	                        node.lineno,
409	                        f"`{node.name}` asserts nothing",
410	                    )
411	                )
412	        elif isinstance(node, ast.ExceptHandler) and _is_trivial(node.body):
413	            if (None if node.type is None else _name(node.type)) in TRIVIAL_EXCEPT:
414	                found.append(
415	                    Finding("swallowed-exception", path, node.lineno, "exception swallowed")
416	                )
417	    return found
418	
419	
420	def open_tasks() -> set[str]:
421	    """Task IDs with an unchecked line in tasks/todo.md (none when there is no todo)."""
422	    path = Path("tasks/todo.md")
423	    return set(OPEN_TASK.findall(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))) if path.is_file() else set()
424	
425	
426	def task_stub_findings(paths: list[str], at_exit: bool) -> list[Finding]:
427	    """Interface stubs anywhere in the project's code: each must name an open task, and at the
428	    milestone exit none may be left. Not diff-scoped: the stub was written by an earlier task."""
429	    open_ids, found = open_tasks(), []
430	    for path in paths:
431	        if not is_python(path) or is_test_file(path) or not Path(path).is_file():
432	            continue
433	        try:
434	            tree = ast.parse(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
435	        except (SyntaxError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):
436	            continue
437	        for node in ast.walk(tree):
438	            if not isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
439	                continue
440	            if not (task := _task_stub(node.body)):
441	                continue
442	            if at_exit:
443	                found.append(
444	                    Finding(
445	                        "stub-left",
446	                        path,
447	                        node.lineno,
448	                        f"`{node.name}` is still the stub for {task}",
449	                    )
450	                )
451	            elif task not in open_ids:
452	                found.append(
453	                    Finding(
454	                        "stub-task",
455	                        path,
456	                        node.lineno,
457	                        f"`{node.name}` is a stub naming {task}, which is not open in tasks/todo.md",
458	                    )
459	                )
460	    return found
461	
462

464	MAX_KEYS = (
465	    "tool.ruff.lint.mccabe.max-complexity",

wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-a2cf415a90b125018/src/rllab/core/types.py
"""Shared types: action spaces, world specs, steps, episodes, protocols, settings and seed sets."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass, fields
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Literal, Protocol

import numpy as np
import numpy.typing as npt

Direction = Literal["higher", "lower"]
Settings = Mapping[str, int | float | str | bool]
Array = npt.NDArray[Any]

EVAL_SEEDS: tuple[int, ...] = tuple(range(1_000_000, 1_000_020))
FINAL_SEEDS: tuple[int, ...] = tuple(range(2_000_000, 2_000_100))


def _same_fields(a: object, b: object) -> bool:
    """Field-by-field equality of two dataclasses, comparing array fields by value."""
    if type(a) is not type(b):
        return False
    for f in fields(a):  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        x, y = getattr(a, f.name), getattr(b, f.name)
        if isinstance(x, np.ndarray) or isinstance(y, np.ndarray):
            if not np.array_equal(x, y):
                return False
        elif x != y:
            return False
    return True


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Discrete:
    """A choice from `n` actions: each action is an int in `[0, n)`."""

    n: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Box:
    """`dims` numbers per action, each in `[low, high]`."""

    dims: int
    low: float
    high: float


ActionSpace = Discrete | Box


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TeamSpec:
    name: str
    agents: tuple[int, ...]
    score_name: str
    direction: Direction


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MeasureSpec:
    name: str
    team: str
    direction: Direction


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class WorldSpec:
    world: str
    variant: str
    agents: tuple[str, ...]
    teams: tuple[TeamSpec, ...]
    obs_len: int
    action: ActionSpace
    max_steps: int
    measures: tuple[MeasureSpec, ...]
    sight: float | None


@dataclass(frozen=True, eq=False)
class Step:
    """One world step for every agent; `done` ends the episode, `truncated` says `max_steps` did."""

    obs: Array
    rewards: Array
    active: Array
    done: bool
    truncated: bool

    def __eq__(self, other: object) -> bool:
        return _same_fields(self, other)

    __hash__ = None  # type: ignore[assignment]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EpisodeResult:
    seed: int
    length: int
    scores: Mapping[str, float]
    team_rewards: Mapping[str, float]
    measures: Mapping[str, float]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Drawable:
    kind: str
    x: float
    y: float
    size: float
    team: str | None
    agent: int | None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DrawState:
    width: float
    height: float
    objects: tuple[Drawable, ...]


@dataclass(frozen=True, eq=False)
class Frame:
    step: int
    draw: DrawState
    scores: Mapping[str, float]
    rewards: Array

    def __eq__(self, other: object) -> bool:
        return _same_fields(self, other)

    __hash__ = None  # type: ignore[assignment]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Episode:
    result: EpisodeResult
    frames: tuple[Frame, ...] | None
    hit_step_limit: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Output:
    path: Path
    kind: Literal["chart", "video", "writeup"]
    made_from: tuple[str, ...]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MethodConfig:
    method: Literal["dqn", "ppo"]
    settings: Settings


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RunConfig:
    name: str
    world: str
    variant: str
    methods: Mapping[str, MethodConfig]
    seed: int
    budget: int
    check_settings: Mapping[str, Settings]
    device: str = "cpu"


class ActionError(ValueError):
    """An action outside the world's declared `ActionSpace`, never clipped."""

    def __init__(self, agent: str, value: object) -> None:
        super().__init__(f"action {value!r} of agent {agent!r} is outside the action space")
        self.agent = agent
        self.value = value


class World(Protocol):
    spec: WorldSpec

    def reset(self, seed: int) -> Step: ...

    def step(self, actions: Array) -> Step: ...

    def result(self) -> EpisodeResult: ...

    def draw(self) -> DrawState: ...


class Policy(Protocol):
    def act(self, obs: Array, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Array: ...

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EpisodeResult:
new
    def __eq__(self, other: object) -> bool:

    def __hash__(self) -> int:
        return hash((self.done, self.truncated))

"""Learner types: one team's transition, the learner protocol and its errors."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Protocol

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import Array, Policy


@dataclass(frozen=True, eq=False)
class Transition:
    """One step of one team; rows are the team's agents in `TeamSpec.agents` order."""

    obs: Array
    actions: Array
    rewards: Array
    next_obs: Array
    done: bool
    truncated: bool
    active: Array


class Learner(Protocol):
    def act(self, obs: Array, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Array: ...

    def observe(self, t: Transition) -> None: ...

    def policy(self) -> Policy: ...

    def state_dict(self) -> dict[str, object]: ...


class NonFiniteError(ArithmeticError):
    """A loss or parameter became NaN or infinite; raised before any parameter changes."""

    def __init__(self, value: float) -> None:
        super().__init__(f"non-finite value {value!r} in a loss or parameter")
        self.value = value


class SpecMismatchError(ValueError):
    """A checkpoint's world spec no longer matches the world; `fields` names each difference."""

    def __init__(self, fields: Sequence[str]) -> None:
        super().__init__(f"checkpoint spec differs from the world in: {', '.join(fields)}")
        self.fields = tuple(fields)

"""Run folder types: the run record, evaluation points, checkpoints, the run view and progress."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal, Protocol

from rllab.core import EpisodeResult, MethodConfig, RunConfig, WorldSpec

RunStatus = Literal["running", "finished", "incomplete", "failed"]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Failure:
    """Where a run stopped on a non-finite number, and its last good checkpoint."""

    step: int
    value: float
    checkpoint: Path


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RunRecord:
    config: RunConfig
    spec: WorldSpec
    started: datetime
    ended: datetime | None
    status: RunStatus
    failure: Failure | None
    path: Path


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EvalPoint:
    step: int
    scores: Mapping[str, float]
    measures: Mapping[str, float]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Checkpoint:
    run: str
    pct: int
    step: int
    spec: WorldSpec
    methods: Mapping[str, MethodConfig]
    state: Mapping[str, dict[str, object]]
    path: Path


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Progress:
    step: int
    budget: int
    latest: EvalPoint | None
    elapsed: timedelta
    status: str


class Run(Protocol):
    """A read-only view of one run folder, readable while the run writes (LOG-06)."""

    record: RunRecord

    def episodes(self) -> list[EpisodeResult]: ...

    def evals(self) -> list[EvalPoint]: ...

    def checkpoints(self) -> list[Checkpoint]: ...


class RunExistsError(FileExistsError):
    """The run folder `runs/<world>/<variant>/<name>` already exists; nothing was written."""

    def __init__(self, path: Path) -> None:
        super().__init__(f"run folder {path} already exists")
        self.path = path

"""The run folder writer (AD-06): record, JSON Lines logs and checkpoints."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from pathlib import Path

from rllab.core import EpisodeResult, RunConfig, WorldSpec
from rllab.runlog.types import Checkpoint, EvalPoint, Failure, RunRecord


class RunWriter:
    def __init__(self, root: Path, config: RunConfig, spec: WorldSpec) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T09")

    def episode(self, result: EpisodeResult) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T09")

"""Training types: the method check's result and the batch's refusal."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CheckResult:
    """`agreement` is the share of check-world states where the policy picks the best action."""

    method: str
    world: str
    agreement: float
    passed: bool


class CheckFailedError(RuntimeError):
    """A method failed its check world, so the batch starts no run (TRAIN-06)."""

    def __init__(self, result: CheckResult) -> None:
        super().__init__(
            f"{result.method} failed its check on {result.world}: "
            f"agreement {result.agreement:.2f} < 0.95"
        )
        self.result = result

"""Chart errors."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence


class MixedRunsError(ValueError):
    """The runs of one chart differ by more than their seed; `settings` names each difference."""

    def __init__(self, settings: Sequence[str]) -> None:
        super().__init__(f"runs differ by more than seed in: {', '.join(settings)}")
        self.settings = tuple(settings)

"""Final evaluation types: baseline comparisons, the verdict and the per-team summary."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Literal


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Comparison:
    """One paired permutation test; `difference` is positive when the learned agent is better."""

    baseline: Literal["random", "rule"]
    team: str
    learned_mean: float
    baseline_mean: float
    difference: float
    p_raw: float
    p_holm: float
    rejected: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Verdict:
    beats_baselines: bool
    failing_runs: tuple[str, ...]
    runs_needed: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Summary:
    world: str
    variant: str
    team: str
    runs: tuple[str, ...]
    learned_mean: float
    learned_range: tuple[float, float]
    run_means: Mapping[str, float]
    random_mean: float
    rule_mean: float
    comparisons: tuple[Comparison, ...]
    verdict: Verdict
    collapse: int | None

"""The `rllab` command: parses arguments, calls one pipeline entry, prints its result (AD-10)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import sys
from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

from rllab.cli.pipelines import run_chart, run_evaluate, run_watch
from rllab.train import run_batch, run_check


def parse_seeds(text: str) -> list[int]:
    """`1-5` or `1,3,7` to a list of seeds."""
    try:
        if "-" in text:
            first, last = (int(part) for part in text.split("-", 1))
            seeds = list(range(first, last + 1))
        else:
            seeds = [int(part) for part in text.split(",")]
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError(f"seeds must look like 1-5 or 1,3,7: {text!r}") from exc
    if not seeds:
        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError(f"no seeds in {text!r}")
    return seeds


def _check(args: argparse.Namespace) -> object:
    return run_check(args.config, args.seed)


def _train(args: argparse.Namespace) -> object:
    return run_batch(args.config, [args.seed], args.project / "runs")


def _batch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> object:
    return run_batch(args.config, args.seeds, args.project / "runs", workers=args.workers)


def _watch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> object:
    return run_watch(args.run_path)


def _chart(args: argparse.Namespace) -> object:
    return run_chart(
        args.project, args.world, args.variant, include=args.include, measure=args.measure
    )


def _evaluate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> object:
    return run_evaluate(args.project, args.world, args.variant, include=args.include)


def _parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="rllab")
    parser.add_argument("--project", type=Path, default=Path(), help="holds runs/ and reports/")
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)

    check = sub.add_parser("check", help="train each team method on its check world")
    check.add_argument("config", type=Path)
    check.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
    check.set_defaults(run=_check)

    train = sub.add_parser("train", help="one training run: a one-seed batch")
    train.add_argument("config", type=Path)
    train.add_argument("--seed", type=int, required=True)
    train.set_defaults(run=_train)

    batch = sub.add_parser("batch", help="the method check, then one run per seed in parallel")
    batch.add_argument("config", type=Path)
    batch.add_argument("--seeds", type=parse_seeds, required=True)
    batch.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=None)
    batch.set_defaults(run=_batch)

    watch = sub.add_parser("watch", help="one progress snapshot of a run")
    watch.add_argument("run_path", type=Path)
    watch.set_defaults(run=_watch)

    for name, run, text in (
        ("chart", _chart, "the learning-curve chart of a world and variant"),
        ("evaluate", _evaluate, "the final evaluation of a world and variant"),
    ):
        command = sub.add_parser(name, help=text)
        command.add_argument("world")
        command.add_argument("variant")
        command.add_argument("--include", action="append", default=[], metavar="NAME")
        command.set_defaults(run=run)
    sub.choices["chart"].add_argument("--measure", default=None)
    return parser


def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
    """Run one `rllab` subcommand; every error is printed as one line, exit code 1."""
    args = _parser().parse_args(argv)
    try:
        result = args.run(args)
    except Exception as exc:  # the one place errors become an exit code (cli.main contract)
        print(f"rllab {args.command}: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    for item in result if isinstance(result, list) else [result]:
        print(item)
    return 0

"""Load the frozen contract fixtures: samples, schemas, replay doubles and fakes.

Each `tests/contracts/<component>.<Symbol>/` folder holds `schema.json` and `samples/*.json` for a
type, `double.py` (plus its recordings) for a Protocol, or `fake.py` for a boundary function.
Folder names hold a dot, so nothing here is an importable package: load this module by path, once,
under the name `contracts_load` (doubles and fakes import it by that name):

    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("contracts_load", ROOT / "load.py")
    load = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules["contracts_load"] = load
    spec.loader.exec_module(load)

then `load.sample("core.WorldSpec", "snake_default")`, `load.double("core.World").make()` or
`load.fake("worlds.make").make("snake")`.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
import importlib.util
import json
import re
import sys
import types
import typing
from collections.abc import Mapping
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Literal, Union, get_args, get_origin, get_type_hints

import numpy as np

from rllab import baselines, core, evaluate, methods, runlog, train

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent

# Each type folder's Python type: samples decode into it.
TYPES: dict[str, Any] = {
    "core.ActionSpace": core.ActionSpace,
    "core.TeamSpec": core.TeamSpec,
    "core.WorldSpec": core.WorldSpec,
    "core.Step": core.Step,
    "core.EpisodeResult": core.EpisodeResult,
    "core.DrawState": core.DrawState,
    "core.Frame": core.Frame,
    "core.Episode": core.Episode,
    "core.play_episode": core.Episode,
    "core.EVAL_SEEDS": tuple[int, ...],
    "core.FINAL_SEEDS": tuple[int, ...],
    "core.Output": core.Output,
    "core.MethodConfig": core.MethodConfig,
    "core.RunConfig": core.RunConfig,
    "baselines.BaselineMeans": baselines.BaselineMeans,
    "methods.Transition": methods.Transition,
    "train.CheckResult": train.CheckResult,
    "runlog.RunRecord": runlog.RunRecord,
    "runlog.EvalPoint": runlog.EvalPoint,
    "runlog.Checkpoint": runlog.Checkpoint,
    "runlog.Progress": runlog.Progress,
    "evaluate.Comparison": evaluate.Comparison,
    "evaluate.Summary": evaluate.Summary,
}


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- files
def read(symbol: str, name: str) -> Any:
    """The JSON file `name` in `symbol`'s folder."""
    return json.loads((ROOT / symbol / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def sample(symbol: str, name: str) -> Any:
    """Sample `name` of `symbol`, decoded into its type."""
    return decode(read(symbol, f"samples/{name}.json"), TYPES[symbol])


def sample_names(symbol: str) -> list[str]:
    return sorted(p.stem for p in (ROOT / symbol / "samples").glob("*.json"))


def module(path: Path) -> types.ModuleType:
    """The Python file at `path`, loaded once and kept in `sys.modules`."""
    name = "contracts_" + re.sub(r"\W", "_", str(path.relative_to(ROOT).with_suffix("")))
    if name in sys.modules:
        return sys.modules[name]
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name, path)
    assert spec is not None and spec.loader is not None, path
    loaded = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules[name] = loaded
    spec.loader.exec_module(loaded)
    return loaded


def double(symbol: str) -> types.ModuleType:
    return module(ROOT / symbol / "double.py")


def fake(symbol: str) -> types.ModuleType:
    return module(ROOT / symbol / "fake.py")


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------- decode JSON
def _decode_union(value: Any, args: tuple[Any, ...]) -> Any:
    if value is None and type(None) in args:
        return None
    problems = []
    for arg in args:
        if arg is type(None):
            continue
        try:
            return decode(value, arg)
        except (TypeError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
            problems.append(f"{arg}: {exc}")
    raise ValueError(f"{value!r} matches none of {args}: {problems}")


def _decode_primitive(value: Any, tp: Any) -> Any:
    if tp is bool and isinstance(value, bool):
        return value
    if tp is int and isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool):
        return value
    if tp is float and isinstance(value, int | float) and not isinstance(value, bool):
        return float(value)
    if tp is str and isinstance(value, str):
        return value
    raise TypeError(f"{value!r} is not a {tp.__name__}")


def _decode_array(value: Any) -> np.ndarray:
    if not isinstance(value, list):
        raise TypeError(f"{value!r} is not an array")
    array = np.asarray(value)
    return array.astype(np.float32) if array.dtype.kind == "f" else array


def _decode_dataclass(value: Any, tp: Any) -> Any:
    if not isinstance(value, dict):
        raise TypeError(f"{value!r} is not an object for {tp.__name__}")
    hints = get_type_hints(tp)
    names = {f.name for f in dataclasses.fields(tp)}
    required = {
        f.name
        for f in dataclasses.fields(tp)
        if f.default is dataclasses.MISSING and f.default_factory is dataclasses.MISSING
    }
    if unknown := set(value) - names:
        raise KeyError(f"{tp.__name__} has no field {sorted(unknown)}")
    if missing := required - set(value):
        raise KeyError(f"{tp.__name__} needs {sorted(missing)}")
    return tp(**{k: decode(v, hints[k]) for k, v in value.items()})


def decode(value: Any, tp: Any) -> Any:
    """JSON `value` as an instance of `tp`: dataclasses, tuples, mappings, unions, arrays."""
    origin, args = get_origin(tp), get_args(tp)
    if tp is Any or tp is object:
        return value
    if origin in (Union, types.UnionType):
        return _decode_union(value, args)
    if origin is Literal:
        if value not in args:
            raise ValueError(f"{value!r} is not one of {args}")
        return value
    if tp in (bool, int, float, str):
        return _decode_primitive(value, tp)
    if tp is Path:
        return Path(_decode_primitive(value, str))
    if tp is datetime:
        return datetime.fromisoformat(_decode_primitive(value, str))
    if tp is timedelta:
        return timedelta(seconds=_decode_primitive(value, float))
    if tp is np.ndarray or origin is np.ndarray:
        return _decode_array(value)
    if origin is tuple:
        if not isinstance(value, list):
            raise TypeError(f"{value!r} is not an array")
        if len(args) == 2 and args[1] is Ellipsis:
            return tuple(decode(v, args[0]) for v in value)
        if len(args) != len(value):
            raise ValueError(f"{value!r} needs {len(args)} items")
        return tuple(decode(v, a) for v, a in zip(value, args, strict=True))
    if origin in (Mapping, dict, typing.Mapping):
        if not isinstance(value, dict):
            raise TypeError(f"{value!r} is not an object")
        return {k: decode(v, args[1]) for k, v in value.items()}
    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(tp):
        return _decode_dataclass(value, tp)
    raise TypeError(f"cannot decode into {tp!r}")


def encode(value: Any) -> Any:
    """The JSON form of a contract value: the inverse of `decode`."""
    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(value) and not isinstance(value, type):
        return {f.name: encode(getattr(value, f.name)) for f in dataclasses.fields(value)}
    if isinstance(value, np.ndarray):
        return value.tolist()
    if isinstance(value, np.generic):
        return value.item()
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return {k: encode(v) for k, v in value.items()}
    if isinstance(value, tuple | list):
        return [encode(v) for v in value]
    if isinstance(value, Path):
        return value.as_posix()
    if isinstance(value, datetime):
        return value.isoformat()
    if isinstance(value, timedelta):
        return value.total_seconds()
    return value


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------ JSON Schema
_JSON_TYPES: dict[str, Any] = {
    "object": dict,
    "array": list,
    "string": str,
    "number": int | float,
    "integer": int,
    "boolean": bool,
    "null": type(None),
}


def _is_type(value: Any, name: str) -> bool:
    if isinstance(value, bool) and name in ("number", "integer"):
        return False
    return isinstance(value, _JSON_TYPES[name])


def _resolve(ref: str, base: Path) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], Path]:
    file, _, pointer = ref.partition("#")
    path = (base.parent / file).resolve() if file else base
    node: Any = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    for part in filter(None, pointer.split("/")):
        node = node[part]
    return node, path


def _check_keywords(value: Any, schema: dict[str, Any], where: str) -> list[str]:
    errors = []
    if "type" in schema:
        names = schema["type"] if isinstance(schema["type"], list) else [schema["type"]]
        if not any(_is_type(value, n) for n in names):
            errors.append(f"{where}: {value!r} is not of type {names}")
    if "enum" in schema and value not in schema["enum"]:
        errors.append(f"{where}: {value!r} is not one of {schema['enum']}")
    if "const" in schema and value != schema["const"]:
        errors.append(f"{where}: {value!r} is not {schema['const']!r}")
    if _is_type(value, "number"):
        if "minimum" in schema and value < schema["minimum"]:
            errors.append(f"{where}: {value} < {schema['minimum']}")
        if "maximum" in schema and value > schema["maximum"]:
            errors.append(f"{where}: {value} > {schema['maximum']}")
    if isinstance(value, list):
        if len(value) < schema.get("minItems", 0):
            errors.append(f"{where}: fewer than {schema['minItems']} items")
        if len(value) > schema.get("maxItems", len(value)):
            errors.append(f"{where}: more than {schema['maxItems']} items")
    return errors


def _check_children(value: Any, schema: dict[str, Any], base: Path, where: str) -> list[str]:
    errors = []
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        properties = schema.get("properties", {})
        errors += [f"{where}: missing {k!r}" for k in schema.get("required", []) if k not in value]
        for key, item in value.items():
            if key in properties:
                errors += validate(item, properties[key], base, f"{where}.{key}")
            elif schema.get("additionalProperties") is False:
                errors.append(f"{where}: unexpected {key!r}")
            elif isinstance(schema.get("additionalProperties"), dict):
                errors += validate(item, schema["additionalProperties"], base, f"{where}.{key}")
    if isinstance(value, list):
        prefix = schema.get("prefixItems", [])
        for i, item in enumerate(value):
            sub = prefix[i] if i < len(prefix) else schema.get("items")
            if isinstance(sub, dict):
                errors += validate(item, sub, base, f"{where}[{i}]")
    return errors


def validate(value: Any, schema: dict[str, Any], base: Path, where: str = "$") -> list[str]:
    """Problems with `value` against `schema` (a JSON Schema subset); `$ref`s resolve from `base`."""
    if "$ref" in schema:
        target, path = _resolve(schema["$ref"], base)
        return validate(value, target, path, where)
    if "anyOf" in schema:
        options = [validate(value, s, base, where) for s in schema["anyOf"]]
        if all(options):
            return [f"{where}: matches no anyOf option: {options}"]
    return _check_keywords(value, schema, where) + _check_children(value, schema, base, where)


def schema_problems(symbol: str, value: Any) -> list[str]:
    """Problems with JSON `value` against `symbol`'s `schema.json`."""
    path = ROOT / symbol / "schema.json"
    return validate(value, json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")), path)

80	def module(path: Path) -> types.ModuleType:
81	    """The Python file at `path`, loaded once and kept in `sys.modules`."""
82	    name = "contracts_" + re.sub(r"\W", "_", str(path.relative_to(ROOT).with_suffix("")))
83	    if name in sys.modules:
84	        return sys.modules[name]
85	    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name, path)
86	    assert spec is not None and spec.loader is not None, path
87	    loaded = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
88	    sys.modules[name] = loaded
89	    spec.loader.exec_module(loaded)
90	    return loaded

101	# ------------------------------------------------------------------------- decode JSON
102	def _decode_union(value: Any, args: tuple[Any, ...]) -> Any:
103	    if value is None and type(None) in args:
104	        return None
105	    problems = []
106	    for arg in args:
107	        if arg is type(None):
108	            continue
109	        try:
110	            return decode(value, arg)
111	        except (TypeError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
112	            problems.append(f"{arg}: {exc}")
113	    raise ValueError(f"{value!r} matches none of {args}: {problems}")
114

116	def _decode_primitive(value: Any, tp: Any) -> Any:
117	    if tp is bool and isinstance(value, bool):
118	        return value
119	    if tp is int and isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool):
120	        return value
121	    if tp is float and isinstance(value, int | float) and not isinstance(value, bool):
122	        return float(value)
123	    if tp is str and isinstance(value, str):
124	        return value
125	    raise TypeError(f"{value!r} is not a {tp.__name__}")
126

128	def _decode_array(value: Any) -> np.ndarray:
129	    if not isinstance(value, list):
130	        raise TypeError(f"{value!r} is not an array")
131	    array = np.asarray(value)
132	    return array.astype(np.float32) if array.dtype.kind == "f" else array
133

135	def _decode_dataclass(value: Any, tp: Any) -> Any:
136	    if not isinstance(value, dict):
137	        raise TypeError(f"{value!r} is not an object for {tp.__name__}")
138	    hints = get_type_hints(tp)
139	    names = {f.name for f in dataclasses.fields(tp)}
140	    required = {
141	        f.name
142	        for f in dataclasses.fields(tp)
143	        if f.default is dataclasses.MISSING and f.default_factory is dataclasses.MISSING
144	    }
145	    if unknown := set(value) - names:
146	        raise KeyError(f"{tp.__name__} has no field {sorted(unknown)}")
147	    if missing := required - set(value):
148	        raise KeyError(f"{tp.__name__} needs {sorted(missing)}")
149	    return tp(**{k: decode(v, hints[k]) for k, v in value.items()})

152	def decode(value: Any, tp: Any) -> Any:
153	    """JSON `value` as an instance of `tp`: dataclasses, tuples, mappings, unions, arrays."""
154	    origin, args = get_origin(tp), get_args(tp)
155	    if tp is Any or tp is object:
156	        return value
157	    if origin in (Union, types.UnionType):
158	        return _decode_union(value, args)
159	    if origin is Literal:
160	        if value not in args:
161	            raise ValueError(f"{value!r} is not one of {args}")
162	        return value
163	    if tp in (bool, int, float, str):
164	        return _decode_primitive(value, tp)
165	    if tp is Path:
166	        return Path(_decode_primitive(value, str))
167	    if tp is datetime:
168	        return datetime.fromisoformat(_decode_primitive(value, str))
169	    if tp is timedelta:
170	        return timedelta(seconds=_decode_primitive(value, float))
171	    if tp is np.ndarray or origin is np.ndarray:
172	        return _decode_array(value)
173	    if origin is tuple:
174	        if not isinstance(value, list):
175	            raise TypeError(f"{value!r} is not an array")
176	        if len(args) == 2 and args[1] is Ellipsis:
177	            return tuple(decode(v, args[0]) for v in value)
178	        if len(args) != len(value):
179	            raise ValueError(f"{value!r} needs {len(args)} items")
180	        return tuple(decode(v, a) for v, a in zip(value, args, strict=True))
181	    if origin in (Mapping, dict, typing.Mapping):
182	        if not isinstance(value, dict):
183	            raise TypeError(f"{value!r} is not an object")
184	        return {k: decode(v, args[1]) for k, v in value.items()}
185	    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(tp):
186	        return _decode_dataclass(value, tp)
187	    raise TypeError(f"cannot decode into {tp!r}")

190	def encode(value: Any) -> Any:
191	    """The JSON form of a contract value: the inverse of `decode`."""
192	    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(value) and not isinstance(value, type):
193	        return {f.name: encode(getattr(value, f.name)) for f in dataclasses.fields(value)}
