"""Fake `train.run_train`: writes the `runlog.Run` double's finished 3-checkpoint snake run.

The folder is `root/<world>/<variant>/<name>` (AD-06): `run.json`, `episodes.jsonl`,
`evals.jsonl` and `checkpoints/ckpt_<pct>.pt` (a `torch.save` of the checkpoint's JSON form).
An existing folder raises `RunExistsError` before anything is written.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
import json
from pathlib import Path

import torch
from contracts_load import double, encode

from rllab.core import RunConfig
from rllab.runlog import RunExistsError, RunRecord


def run_train(config: RunConfig, root: Path) -> RunRecord:
    path = root / config.world / config.variant / config.name
    if path.exists():
        raise RunExistsError(path)
    run = double("runlog.Run").make(name=config.name)
    record = dataclasses.replace(run.record, config=config, path=path)
    (path / "checkpoints").mkdir(parents=True)
    lines = {"episodes.jsonl": run.episodes(), "evals.jsonl": run.evals()}
    for name, rows in lines.items():
        text = "".join(json.dumps(encode(row)) + "\n" for row in rows)
        (path / name).write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
    for ckpt in run.checkpoints():
        torch.save(encode(dataclasses.replace(ckpt, path=path / "checkpoints" / ckpt.path.name)), path / "checkpoints" / ckpt.path.name)
    (path / "run.json").write_text(json.dumps(encode(record), indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
    return record

from pathlib import Path

def run_batch(
    config_path: Path,
    seeds: Sequence[int],
    root: Path,
    *,
    workers: int | None = None,
    check_seed: int = 0,
) -> list[RunRecord]:

    records = []
    for seed in seeds:

        config = dataclasses.replace(finished.config, name=name, seed=seed)

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

from contracts_load import double

from rllab.runlog import Run

INCOMPLETE = "snake-default-s6"


def find_runs(root: Path, world: str, variant: str, *, include: Sequence[str] = ()) -> list[Run]:
    if (world, variant) != ("snake", "default"):
        return []
    run_double = double("runlog.Run")
    runs: list[Run] = [run_double.make(name=f"snake-default-s{i}") for i in range(1, 6)]
    if INCOMPLETE in include:
        runs.append(run_double.make(name=INCOMPLETE, status="incomplete"))
    return runs

from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

from rllab.baselines import BaselineMeans
from rllab.core import Output
from rllab.runlog import Run

PNG_SIGNATURE = b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n"


def learning_curve(
    runs: Sequence[Run], baselines: BaselineMeans, out: Path, *, measure: str | None = None
) -> Output:
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out.write_bytes(PNG_SIGNATURE)
    return Output(out, "chart", tuple(run.record.config.name for run in runs))

"""Registers `load.py` as `contracts_load` before the contract tests are collected."""

import importlib.util
import sys
from pathlib import Path

if "contracts_load" not in sys.modules:
    _spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
        "contracts_load", Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "load.py"
    )
    assert _spec is not None
    assert _spec.loader is not None
    _module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(_spec)
    sys.modules["contracts_load"] = _module
    _spec.loader.exec_module(_module)

wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-a2cf415a90b125018/tests/contracts/test_samples.py
"""Every type sample validates against its schema and decodes into its frozen type."""

import dataclasses
import json

import pytest
from contracts_load import ROOT, TYPES, decode, encode, read, sample, sample_names, schema_problems

from rllab import core

SAMPLES = [(symbol, name) for symbol in TYPES for name in sample_names(symbol)]


def test_every_type_folder_has_a_schema_and_a_sample() -> None:
    for symbol in TYPES:
        assert (ROOT / symbol / "schema.json").is_file(), symbol
        assert sample_names(symbol), symbol


def test_every_contract_folder_is_a_type_double_or_fake() -> None:
    for folder in (p for p in ROOT.iterdir() if p.is_dir() and "." in p.name):
        kinds = [
            folder.name in TYPES,
            (folder / "double.py").is_file(),
            (folder / "fake.py").is_file(),
        ]
        assert sum(kinds) == 1, folder.name


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("symbol", "name"), SAMPLES)
def test_sample_matches_its_schema(symbol: str, name: str) -> None:
    assert schema_problems(symbol, read(symbol, f"samples/{name}.json")) == []


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("symbol", "name"), SAMPLES)
def test_sample_decodes_into_its_type_and_back(symbol: str, name: str) -> None:
    raw = read(symbol, f"samples/{name}.json")
    value = sample(symbol, name)
    if isinstance(TYPES[symbol], type):
        assert isinstance(value, TYPES[symbol])
    assert json.dumps(encode(value), sort_keys=True) == json.dumps(raw, sort_keys=True)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("symbol", [s for s in TYPES if s not in ("core.EVAL_SEEDS", "core.FINAL_SEEDS")])
def test_schema_rejects_a_sample_with_an_unknown_field(symbol: str) -> None:
    raw = read(symbol, f"samples/{sample_names(symbol)[0]}.json")
    if isinstance(raw, dict):
        assert schema_problems(symbol, raw | {"unexpected": 1})
    with pytest.raises((TypeError, KeyError, ValueError)):
        decode(raw | {"unexpected": 1} if isinstance(raw, dict) else "not an object", TYPES[symbol])


def test_schema_rejects_a_missing_field_and_a_wrong_type() -> None:
    spec = read("core.WorldSpec", "samples/snake_default.json")
    assert schema_problems("core.WorldSpec", {k: v for k, v in spec.items() if k != "obs_len"})
    assert schema_problems("core.WorldSpec", spec | {"obs_len": "12"})
    assert schema_problems("core.WorldSpec", spec | {"action": {"n": 3, "dims": 2}})
    assert schema_problems("core.TeamSpec", read("core.TeamSpec", "samples/snake.json") | {"direction": "up"})


def test_seed_sets_match_the_contract_and_are_disjoint() -> None:
    assert sample("core.EVAL_SEEDS", "seeds") == core.EVAL_SEEDS
    assert sample("core.FINAL_SEEDS", "seeds") == core.FINAL_SEEDS
    assert len(core.EVAL_SEEDS) == 20
    assert len(core.FINAL_SEEDS) == 100
    assert min(core.EVAL_SEEDS) >= 1_000_000
    assert not set(core.EVAL_SEEDS) & set(core.FINAL_SEEDS)


def test_action_space_samples_are_the_snake_and_fish_formats() -> None:
    assert sample("core.ActionSpace", "snake") == core.Discrete(3)
    assert sample("core.ActionSpace", "fish") == core.Box(2, -1.0, 1.0)


def test_world_spec_teams_cover_every_agent_once() -> None:
    for name in sample_names("core.WorldSpec"):
        spec = sample("core.WorldSpec", name)
        agents = sorted(i for team in spec.teams for i in team.agents)
        assert agents == list(range(len(spec.agents))), name
        assert {m.team for m in spec.measures} <= {t.name for t in spec.teams}, name


def test_step_samples_zero_the_rows_of_inactive_agents() -> None:
    for name in sample_names("core.Step"):
        step = sample("core.Step", name)
        assert step.obs.dtype == "float32"
        assert step.active.dtype == bool
        assert step.obs.shape[0] == step.rewards.shape[0] == step.active.shape[0]
        assert not step.obs[~step.active].any()
        assert not step.rewards[~step.active].any()


def test_steps_and_frames_compare_arrays_by_value() -> None:
    step = sample("core.Step", "snake_mid_episode")
    assert step == sample("core.Step", "snake_mid_episode")
    assert step != dataclasses.replace(step, rewards=step.rewards + 1)
    frame = sample("core.Frame", "snake_seed7_frame1")
    assert frame == sample("core.Frame", "snake_seed7_frame1")
    assert frame != sample("core.Frame", "snake_seed7_frame0")


def test_traced_episodes_keep_length_plus_one_frames() -> None:
    for symbol, name in (("core.Episode", "check_traced_3_steps"), ("core.play_episode", "snake_rule_seed7_traced")):
        episode = sample(symbol, name)
        assert len(episode.frames) == episode.result.length + 1
        assert [f.step for f in episode.frames] == list(range(episode.result.length + 1))
        assert not episode.frames[0].rewards.any()
    assert sample("core.Episode", "snake_untraced").frames is None


def test_run_samples_hold_full_core_settings() -> None:
    finished = sample("runlog.RunRecord", "snake_finished")
    assert isinstance(finished.config, core.RunConfig)
    assert all(isinstance(m, core.MethodConfig) for m in finished.config.methods.values())
    failed = sample("runlog.RunRecord", "failed_nan")
    assert failed.status == "failed"
    assert failed.failure.step == 41_200
    assert failed.failure.value != failed.failure.value  # NaN
    assert sample("runlog.RunRecord", "incomplete").ended is None
    for name in sample_names("runlog.Checkpoint"):
        assert sample("runlog.Checkpoint", name).pct in range(0, 101, 10)


def test_check_result_samples_pass_at_95_percent() -> None:
    for name in sample_names("train.CheckResult"):
        result = sample("train.CheckResult", name)
        assert result.passed == (result.agreement >= 0.95)


def test_summary_samples_follow_the_verdict_invariants() -> None:
    three = sample("evaluate.Summary", "snake_3_runs")
    assert three.comparisons == ()
    assert three.verdict.runs_needed == 5 - len(three.runs)
    passing = sample("evaluate.Summary", "snake_pass")
    assert passing.verdict.beats_baselines
    assert {c.baseline for c in passing.comparisons} == {"random", "rule"}
    failing = sample("evaluate.Summary", "snake_failing_run")
    assert failing.verdict.failing_runs == tuple(r for r, m in failing.run_means.items() if m <= failing.rule_mean)

new
def folder(symbol: str) -> Path:
    """`symbol`'s folder. Matched without case: `runlog.progress`'s fake shares the
    `runlog.Progress` folder, since the two names are one folder on a case-insensitive disk."""
    return next(p for p in ROOT.iterdir() if p.name.lower() == symbol.lower())


def double(symbol: str) -> types.ModuleType:
    return module(folder(symbol) / "double.py")


def fake(symbol: str) -> types.ModuleType:
    return module(folder(symbol) / "fake.py")

U11.S173Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-a2cf415a90b125018/tests/contracts/test_doubles.py (177 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-a2cf415a90b125018/tests/contracts/test_doubles.py
"""Each Protocol's replay double serves its recording, and refuses what the recording lacks."""

import dataclasses
import re
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pytest
from contracts_load import double, read

from rllab.core import ActionError, Box, Discrete, DrawState, Step
from rllab.runlog import RunRecord

RNG = np.random.default_rng(0)


def test_world_double_replays_the_recorded_seed_42_snake() -> None:
    world = double("core.World").make()
    recording = read("core.World", "recording.json")
    assert world.spec.world == "snake"
    assert world.spec.max_steps == len(recording["steps"]) == 5
    first = world.reset(42)
    assert isinstance(first, Step)
    assert isinstance(world.draw(), DrawState)
    steps = [world.step(actions) for actions in world.recorded_actions]
    assert [s.done for s in steps] == [False] * 4 + [True]
    assert steps[-1].truncated
    assert world.result().length == 5
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
        world.step(np.array([2]))


def test_world_double_refuses_other_seeds_and_actions() -> None:
    world = double("core.World").make()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="seed 42"):
        world.reset(7)
    world.reset(42)
    recorded = int(world.recorded_actions[0][0])
    with pytest.raises(AssertionError, match="differ"):
        world.step(np.array([(recorded + 1) % 3]))
    with pytest.raises(ActionError) as caught:
        world.step(np.array([3]))
    assert caught.value.agent == "snake-0"
    assert isinstance(caught.value, ValueError)


def test_policy_double_acts_as_recorded_and_refuses_unseen_observations() -> None:
    policy = double("core.Policy").make()
    calls = read("core.Policy", "recording.json")["calls"]
    assert len(calls) == 5
    for call in calls:
        assert policy.act(np.asarray(call["obs"], np.float32), RNG).tolist() == call["actions"]
    with pytest.raises(KeyError):
        policy.act(np.full((1, 12), 9.0, np.float32), RNG)


def test_policy_and_world_doubles_replay_one_episode_together() -> None:
    world, policy = double("core.World").make(), double("core.Policy").make()
    step = world.reset(world.seed)
    length = 0
    while not step.done:
        step = world.step(policy.act(step.obs, RNG))
        length += 1
    assert world.result().length == length


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("name", "space"), [("corridor", Discrete(2)), ("point", Box(1, -1.0, 1.0))])
def test_check_world_doubles_serve_states_best_actions_and_an_episode(name: str, space: object) -> None:
    world = double("worlds.CheckWorld").make(name)
    assert world.spec.action == space
    states, best = world.states(), world.best_actions()
    assert states.shape == (len(best), world.spec.obs_len)
    step = world.reset(world.seed)
    for actions in world.recorded_actions:
        step = world.step(actions)
    assert step.done
    assert world.result().length == 3


def test_corridor_best_actions_are_left_twice_then_right() -> None:
    corridor = double("worlds.CheckWorld").make("corridor")
    assert corridor.best_actions().tolist() == [0, 0, 1, 1, 1, 1]
    assert corridor.states().tolist() == np.eye(6).tolist()


def test_point_best_push_is_the_sign_of_the_offset() -> None:
    point = double("worlds.CheckWorld").make("point")
    assert np.array_equal(point.best_actions(), np.sign(point.states()))


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", ["snake", "fish_no_ball"])
def test_rule_doubles_have_one_sentence_and_act(name: str) -> None:
    rule = double("baselines.RulePolicy").make(name)
    assert re.fullmatch(r"[A-Z][^.]*\.", rule.description)
    for obs, actions in rule.calls:
        assert np.array_equal(rule.act(obs, RNG), actions)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", ["snake", "fish"])
def test_random_doubles_serve_five_draws_inside_the_action_space(name: str) -> None:
    random = double("baselines.RandomPolicy").make(name)
    draws = [random.act(np.zeros((1, 1), np.float32), RNG) for _ in range(5)]
    for draw in draws:
        if isinstance(random.action, Discrete):
            assert draw.shape == (1,)
            assert 0 <= draw[0] < random.action.n
        else:
            assert draw.shape == (1, random.action.dims)
            assert np.all((random.action.low <= draw) & (draw <= random.action.high))
    with pytest.raises(IndexError):
        random.act(np.zeros((1, 1), np.float32), RNG)


def test_learner_double_replays_ten_snake_transitions() -> None:
    learner = double("methods.Learner").make()
    assert learner.config.method == "dqn"
    assert len(learner.transitions) == 10
    for t in learner.transitions:
        assert np.array_equal(learner.act(t.obs, RNG), t.actions)
        learner.observe(t)
    assert learner.observed == 10
    greedy = learner.policy()
    assert np.array_equal(greedy.act(learner.transitions[0].obs, RNG), learner.transitions[0].actions)
    assert learner.state_dict() == {"updates": 10, "epsilon": 0.9998}


def test_learner_double_refuses_a_transition_that_differs() -> None:
    learner = double("methods.Learner").make()
    first = learner.transitions[0]
    with pytest.raises(AssertionError, match="rewards"):
        learner.observe(dataclasses.replace(first, rewards=first.rewards + 1))


def test_run_double_serves_a_finished_three_checkpoint_run() -> None:
    run = double("runlog.Run").make()
    assert isinstance(run.record, RunRecord)
    assert run.record.status == "finished"
    assert [c.pct for c in run.checkpoints()] == [0, 50, 100]
    assert [p.step for p in run.evals()] == list(range(0, run.record.config.budget + 1, 100))
    assert sum(e.length for e in run.episodes()) == run.record.config.budget


def test_run_double_renames_and_changes_status() -> None:
    run = double("runlog.Run").make(name="snake-default-s6", status="incomplete")
    assert run.record.config.name == "snake-default-s6"
    assert run.record.path == Path("runs/snake/default/snake-default-s6")
    assert run.record.status == "incomplete"
    assert run.record.ended is None
    assert all(c.run == "snake-default-s6" for c in run.checkpoints())


def test_run_writer_double_takes_the_recorded_writes_in_order() -> None:
    run = double("runlog.Run").make()
    writer = double("runlog.RunWriter").ReplayRunWriter(Path("runs"), run.record.config, run.record.spec)
    episodes, by_step = iter(run.episodes()), {c.step: c for c in run.checkpoints()}
    done, episode = 0, next(episodes, None)
    for point in run.evals():
        while episode is not None and done + episode.length <= point.step:
            writer.episode(episode)
            done += episode.length
            episode = next(episodes, None)
        writer.eval(point)
        if point.step in by_step:
            ckpt = by_step[point.step]
            assert writer.checkpoint(ckpt.pct, ckpt.step, ckpt.state) == ckpt
    assert writer.close("finished") == run.record
    assert writer.remaining() == 0


def test_run_writer_double_refuses_an_unrecorded_call() -> None:
    run = double("runlog.Run").make()
    writer = double("runlog.RunWriter").ReplayRunWriter(Path("runs"), run.record.config, run.record.spec)
    with pytest.raises(AssertionError, match="differs"):
        writer.close("finished")
    other = dataclasses.replace(run.record.config, seed=2)
    with pytest.raises(AssertionError, match="config"):
        double("runlog.RunWriter").ReplayRunWriter(Path("runs"), other, run.record.spec)

"""Each boundary function's fake has the contract signature and serves its fixtures."""

import inspect
import json
from collections.abc import Callable
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pytest
import torch
from contracts_load import double, fake, sample

from rllab import baselines, charts, evaluate, methods, runlog, train, worlds
from rllab.core import Discrete
from rllab.runlog import RunExistsError

FAKES: dict[str, Callable[..., object]] = {
    "worlds.make": worlds.make,
    "baselines.rule_for": baselines.rule_for,
    "baselines.baseline_means": baselines.baseline_means,
    "methods.make_learner": methods.make_learner,
    "methods.load_policy": methods.load_policy,
    "train.load_run_configs": train.load_run_configs,
    "train.run_check": train.run_check,
    "train.run_train": train.run_train,
    "train.run_batch": train.run_batch,
    "runlog.find_runs": runlog.find_runs,
    "runlog.open_run": runlog.open_run,
    "runlog.progress": runlog.progress,
    "charts.learning_curve": charts.learning_curve,
    "evaluate.final_evaluation": evaluate.final_evaluation,
}


def fake_of(symbol: str) -> Callable[..., object]:
    function: Callable[..., object] = getattr(fake(symbol), symbol.split(".")[1])
    return function


def shape(function: Callable[..., object]) -> list[tuple[str, object, object, object]]:
    params = inspect.signature(function).parameters.values()
    return [(p.name, p.kind, p.default, p.annotation) for p in params]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("symbol", FAKES)
def test_fake_has_the_contract_signature(symbol: str) -> None:
    assert shape(fake_of(symbol)) == shape(FAKES[symbol])
    assert inspect.signature(fake_of(symbol)).return_annotation == inspect.signature(FAKES[symbol]).return_annotation


def test_make_fake_returns_the_seed_42_snake_double() -> None:
    world = fake_of("worlds.make")("snake")
    assert world.spec.world == "snake"
    assert world.reset(42) is not None
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="fish"):
        fake_of("worlds.make")("fish", "one")


def test_rule_for_fake_returns_the_snake_rule_double() -> None:
    rule = fake_of("baselines.rule_for")("snake", "default", "snake")
    assert rule.description == double("baselines.RulePolicy").make("snake").description
    with pytest.raises(KeyError):
        fake_of("baselines.rule_for")("snake", "default", "fish")


def test_baseline_means_fake_returns_the_snake_sample() -> None:
    assert fake_of("baselines.baseline_means")("snake", "default") == sample("baselines.BaselineMeans", "snake")
    with pytest.raises(KeyError):
        fake_of("baselines.baseline_means")("nowhere", "default")


def test_make_learner_fake_builds_dqn_and_refuses_a_box_world() -> None:
    spec = sample("core.WorldSpec", "snake_default")
    dqn = sample("core.MethodConfig", "snake_dqn_defaults")
    learner = fake_of("methods.make_learner")(dqn, spec, "snake", np.random.default_rng(0))
    assert len(learner.transitions) == 10
    fish = sample("core.WorldSpec", "fish_local")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="dqn"):
        fake_of("methods.make_learner")(dqn, fish, "fish", np.random.default_rng(0))


def test_load_policy_fake_returns_the_rule_policy_and_names_spec_changes() -> None:
    ckpt = sample("runlog.Checkpoint", "snake_100pct")
    policy = fake_of("methods.load_policy")(ckpt, "snake", ckpt.spec)
    calls = double("core.Policy").make().calls
    assert np.array_equal(policy.act(calls[0][0], np.random.default_rng(0)), calls[0][1])
    changed = sample("core.WorldSpec", "fish_local")
    with pytest.raises(methods.SpecMismatchError) as caught:
        fake_of("methods.load_policy")(ckpt, "snake", changed)
    assert caught.value.fields == ("obs_len", "action")


def test_load_run_configs_fake_names_one_config_per_seed() -> None:
    configs = fake_of("train.load_run_configs")(Path("configs/snake-default.toml"), [1, 2])
    assert [c.name for c in configs] == ["snake-default-s1", "snake-default-s2"]
    assert [c.seed for c in configs] == [1, 2]
    assert isinstance(configs[0].methods["snake"].settings["lr"], float)


def test_run_check_fake_returns_the_passing_dqn_check() -> None:
    assert fake_of("train.run_check")(Path("configs/snake-default.toml")) == [sample("train.CheckResult", "dqn_pass")]


def test_run_train_fake_writes_the_finished_run_folder(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    config = sample("runlog.RunRecord", "snake_finished").config
    record = fake_of("train.run_train")(config, tmp_path)
    folder = tmp_path / "snake" / "default" / config.name
    assert record.path == folder
    assert record.status == "finished"
    assert json.loads((folder / "run.json").read_text())["config"]["name"] == config.name
    assert len((folder / "evals.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()) == 21
    assert len((folder / "episodes.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()) == 10
    ckpts = sorted(p.name for p in (folder / "checkpoints").iterdir())
    assert ckpts == ["ckpt_0.pt", "ckpt_100.pt", "ckpt_50.pt"]
    assert torch.load(folder / "checkpoints" / "ckpt_50.pt", weights_only=True)["pct"] == 50
    with pytest.raises(RunExistsError):
        fake_of("train.run_train")(config, tmp_path)


def test_run_batch_fake_returns_one_finished_record_per_seed(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    records = fake_of("train.run_batch")(Path("configs/snake-default.toml"), [1, 2], tmp_path)
    assert [r.config.name for r in records] == ["snake-default-s1", "snake-default-s2"]
    assert all(r.status == "finished" for r in records)
    assert records[1].path == tmp_path / "snake" / "default" / "snake-default-s2"

    named = find(Path("runs"), "snake", "default", include=["snake-default-s6"])
    assert len(named) == 6
    assert named[-1].record.status == "incomplete"
    assert find(Path("runs"), "fish", "one") == []

wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-a2cf415a90b125018/tests/contracts/test_cli.py
"""`cli.main` dispatches each M1 subcommand to its pipeline entry and prints what it returns."""

import argparse
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import pytest
from contracts_load import fake, sample

from rllab.cli import commands, main


class Recorder:
    """Stands in for one pipeline entry: records its call and returns `result`."""

    def __init__(self, result: object) -> None:
        self.result = result
        self.calls: list[tuple[tuple[Any, ...], dict[str, Any]]] = []

    def __call__(self, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> object:
        self.calls.append((args, kwargs))
        return self.result


@pytest.fixture
def entries(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> dict[str, Recorder]:
    records = fake("train.run_batch").run_batch(Path("snake-default.toml"), [1, 2], Path("runs"))
    stubs = {
        "run_check": Recorder([sample("train.CheckResult", "dqn_pass")]),
        "run_batch": Recorder(records),
        "run_watch": Recorder(sample("runlog.Progress", "snake_35pct")),
        "run_chart": Recorder(sample("core.Output", "snake_curve")),
        "run_evaluate": Recorder([sample("evaluate.Summary", "snake_pass")]),
    }
    for name, stub in stubs.items():
        monkeypatch.setattr(commands, name, stub)
    return stubs


def test_check_dispatches_to_run_check_and_prints_each_result(
    entries: dict[str, Recorder], capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str]
) -> None:
    assert main(["check", "configs/snake-default.toml", "--seed", "3"]) == 0
    assert entries["run_check"].calls == [((Path("configs/snake-default.toml"), 3), {})]
    assert capsys.readouterr().out == f"{sample('train.CheckResult', 'dqn_pass')}\n"


def test_train_is_a_one_seed_batch(entries: dict[str, Recorder]) -> None:
    assert main(["--project", "lab", "train", "c.toml", "--seed", "4"]) == 0
    assert entries["run_batch"].calls == [((Path("c.toml"), [4], Path("lab/runs")), {})]


def test_batch_parses_a_seed_range_and_workers(entries: dict[str, Recorder], capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str]) -> None:
    assert main(["batch", "c.toml", "--seeds", "1-5", "--workers", "2"]) == 0
    assert entries["run_batch"].calls == [((Path("c.toml"), [1, 2, 3, 4, 5], Path("runs")), {"workers": 2})]
    assert len(capsys.readouterr().out.splitlines()) == 2


def test_watch_dispatches_to_run_watch(entries: dict[str, Recorder], capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str]) -> None:
    assert main(["watch", "runs/snake/default/snake-default-s1"]) == 0
    assert entries["run_watch"].calls == [((Path("runs/snake/default/snake-default-s1"),), {})]
    assert "step=700" in capsys.readouterr().out


def test_chart_passes_include_and_measure(entries: dict[str, Recorder]) -> None:
    argv = ["chart", "snake", "default", "--include", "snake-default-s6", "--measure", "length"]
    assert main(argv) == 0
    call = ((Path(), "snake", "default"), {"include": ["snake-default-s6"], "measure": "length"})
    assert entries["run_chart"].calls == [call]


def test_evaluate_dispatches_to_run_evaluate(entries: dict[str, Recorder]) -> None:
    assert main(["--project", "lab", "evaluate", "snake", "default"]) == 0
    assert entries["run_evaluate"].calls == [((Path("lab"), "snake", "default"), {"include": []})]


def test_an_error_is_one_line_naming_its_cause_with_exit_code_1(
    entries: dict[str, Recorder], monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str]
) -> None:
    def missing(*_: object) -> object:
        raise FileNotFoundError("configs/none.toml")

    monkeypatch.setattr(commands, "run_check", missing)
    assert main(["check", "configs/none.toml"]) == 1
    captured = capsys.readouterr()
    assert captured.out == ""
    assert captured.err == "rllab check: FileNotFoundError: configs/none.toml\n"


def test_an_unbuilt_entry_is_reported_like_any_error(capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str]) -> None:
    assert main(["watch", "runs/snake/default/snake-default-s1"]) == 1
    assert capsys.readouterr().err.startswith("rllab watch: NotImplementedError: M1-T22")


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("text", "seeds"), [("1-5", [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]), ("3", [3]), ("1,4,7", [1, 4, 7])])
def test_parse_seeds_reads_ranges_and_lists(text: str, seeds: list[int]) -> None:
    assert commands.parse_seeds(text) == seeds


@pytest.mark.parametrize("text", ["a-b", "5-1", ""])
def test_parse_seeds_refuses_what_is_not_seeds(text: str) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(argparse.ArgumentTypeError):
        commands.parse_seeds(text)

full text
I can't fix this from inside the task. The Stop hook runs `scripts/check.sh gate --pkg rllab`, and `check.sh` stops before running any check because `pkg_scope` lists only component names (core, worlds, …, cli), not `rllab`. `dt claim` writes `rllab` to `.claude/.check_pkg`, and `dt land` passes the same `--pkg rllab`, so every attempt fails the same way. That is most likely why the earlier attempts were rejected. Both `scripts/check.sh` and the dt claim/land code are outside this task's Writes, and none of the escalation types fits, so the fix needs a build-setup change by you: add an `rllab` case to `pkg_scope`, or stop passing `--pkg` for a single-package project. Until then `dt land` will reject this task again.

Without `--pkg`, `scripts/check.sh gate` passes on the work itself: lint, types, size, deptry, import-linter, the 185 contract tests and the floor guard. The full code is under `src/rllab/` and `tests/contracts/`.

Choices you may want to review:
- **PL-05 and PL-06 entries:** the pre-build sketches give `run_chart` and `run_evaluate` six arguments, which the five-argument lint limit rejects. They now take one `project` folder (holding `runs/` and `reports/`) instead of separate `root` and `out`, and `run_evaluate` keeps `workers`. `src/rllab/cli/pipelines/pl05_chart.py` and `pl06_evaluate.py` hold them.
- **`runlog.progress` fake:** it shares the `runlog.Progress` folder, because the two names are the same folder on this case-insensitive disk.
- **`tests/contracts/generate.py`:** I couldn't write a temporary script anywhere else or run it inline, so the script that made the samples, recordings and schemas is in the contract folder. It is about 600 lines; the gate doesn't size-check tests, but it is over the usual 500.
